# ============================================================
# TIME HELPERS
# ============================================================

def utc_now():
    return pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")


def iso(ts):
    return pd.Timestamp(ts).tz_convert("UTC").isoformat()


# ============================================================
# STATE
# ============================================================

def new_state():
    return {
        "state_version": STATE_VERSION,
        "strategy": "u67_short_only_frozen_v1",
        "created_at": utc_now().isoformat(),
        "eligible_from": iso(
            max(
                REGISTRATION_ELIGIBLE_FROM,
                utc_now(),
            )
        ),

        # Intentionally NOT updated every run.
        # This prevents 15-minute state churn.
        "last_run_at": None,

        "symbols": {
            s: {
                "last_ts": None,
                "baseline_done": False,
                "last_status": None,
            }
            for s in SYMS
        },

        "open": {},
        "closed": [],
        "signals": [],
        "missed": [],
        "errors": [],
    }


def atomic_save(state):
    directory = os.path.dirname(
        os.path.abspath(STATE_FILE)
    )

    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)

    tmp = STATE_FILE + ".tmp"

    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(
            state,
            f,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True
        )
        f.write("\n")

    os.replace(tmp, STATE_FILE)


def state_fingerprint(state):
    return json.dumps(
        state,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
        ensure_ascii=False,
    )


def load_state():

    if not os.path.exists(STATE_FILE):
        return new_state(), True

    try:
        with open(
            STATE_FILE,
            "r",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            state = json.load(f)

    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"STATE_READ_ERROR: {e}"
        )

    if not isinstance(state, dict):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "STATE_INVALID: root is not an object"
        )

    version = state.get("state_version")

    if version != STATE_VERSION:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "STATE_VERSION_MISMATCH: "
            f"found={version}, expected={STATE_VERSION}. "
            "State will NOT be auto-reset."
        )

    required = [
        "symbols",
        "open",
        "closed",
        "signals",
        "missed",
        "errors",
        "eligible_from",
    ]

    for key in required:
        if key not in state:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"STATE_INVALID: missing key '{key}'"
            )

    for sym in SYMS:
        state["symbols"].setdefault(
            sym,
            {
                "last_ts": None,
                "baseline_done": False,
                "last_status": None,
            }
        )

    state.setdefault("last_run_at", None)

    return state, False


# ============================================================
# KLINE PARSING
# ============================================================

def parse_klines(rows):

    if not rows:
        return pd.DataFrame()

    parsed = []

    for r in rows:

        if not isinstance(r, (list, tuple)):
            continue

        if len(r) < 6:
            continue

        try:
            # KuCoin Futures:
            # time, open, high, low, close, volume, turnover

            t = int(r[0])

            # Normalize seconds -> milliseconds.
            if t < 10_000_000_000:
                t *= 1000

            o = float(r[1])
            h = float(r[2])
            l = float(r[3])
            c = float(r[4])
            v = float(r[5])

        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue

        if not all(
            np.isfinite(x)
            for x in (o, h, l, c, v)
        ):
            continue

        if min(o, h, l, c) <= 0:
            continue

        if v < 0:
            continue

        if h < max(o, c):
            continue

        if l > min(o, c):
            continue

        if l > h:
            continue

        parsed.append(
            (
                t,
                o,
                h,
                l,
                c,
                v,
            )
        )

    if not parsed:
        return pd.DataFrame()

    df = pd.DataFrame(
        parsed,
        columns=[
            "ts_ms",
            "open",
            "high",
            "low",
            "close",
            "volume",
        ],
    )

    df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(
        df["ts_ms"],
        unit="ms",
        utc=True,
    )

    df = (
        df
        .drop_duplicates("ts")
        .sort_values("ts")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    return df[
        [
            "ts",
            "open",
            "high",
            "low",
            "close",
            "volume",
        ]
    ]


# ============================================================
# KUCOIN FETCH
# ============================================================

def fetch_chunk(symbol, start_ms, end_ms):

    params = {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "granularity": GRANULARITY,
        "from": int(start_ms),
        "to": int(end_ms),
    }

    last_error = None

    for attempt in range(1, RETRIES + 1):

        try:

            r = SESSION.get(
                KLINE_URL,
                params=params,
                timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
            )

            if r.status_code == 200:

                payload = r.json()

                if (
                    isinstance(payload, dict)
                    and payload.get("code") == "200000"
                ):
                    return payload.get("data") or []

                last_error = RuntimeError(
                    "HTTP 200 but unexpected payload: "
                    + str(payload)[:180]
                )

            elif r.status_code in (
                429,
                500,
                502,
                503,
                504,
            ):

                last_error = RuntimeError(
                    f"HTTP {r.status_code}"
                )

            else:

                last_error = RuntimeError(
                    f"HTTP {r.status_code}: "
                    f"{r.text[:180]}"
                )

        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e

        if attempt < RETRIES:
            time.sleep(
                min(2 ** (attempt - 1), 6)
            )

    raise RuntimeError(
        f"FETCH_FAILED {symbol}: {last_error}"
    )


def fetch_raw(symbol, now):

    now_ms = int(
        pd.Timestamp(now).timestamp() * 1000
    )

    chunk_ms = (
        (CHUNK - 1)
        * BAR_SECONDS
        * 1000
    )

    rows = []

    newest_end = now_ms

    for _ in range(N_CHUNKS):

        start = (
            newest_end
            - chunk_ms
        )

        data = fetch_chunk(
            symbol,
            start,
            newest_end,
        )

        rows.extend(data)

        if data:

            parsed = parse_klines(data)

            if not parsed.empty:

                oldest = int(
                    parsed["ts"]
                    .iloc[0]
                    .timestamp()
                    * 1000
                )

                # IMPORTANT:
                # Move BACKWARD while preserving overlap.
                # Previous code used oldest - OVERLAP,
                # which creates a gap.
                #
                # oldest + OVERLAP means the next chunk
                # overlaps the previous chunk by OVERLAP bars.
                newest_end = (
                    oldest
                    + (
                        OVERLAP
                        * BAR_SECONDS
                        * 1000
                    )
                )

            else:

                newest_end = (
                    start
                    - (
                        OVERLAP
                        * BAR_SECONDS
                        * 1000
                    )
                )

        else:

            newest_end = (
                start
                - (
                    OVERLAP
                    * BAR_SECONDS
                    * 1000
                )
            )

        time.sleep(PAUSE)

    return parse_klines(rows)


# ============================================================
# COMPLETED CANDLES
# ============================================================

def completed_only(df, now):

    if df.empty:
        return df

    cutoff = pd.Timestamp(now).floor("4h")

    return (
        df[df["ts"] < cutoff]
        .copy()
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )


# ============================================================
# DATA QUALITY
# ============================================================

def check_contiguous(df):

    if len(df) < MIN_CANDLES:
        return (
            False,
            f"INSUFFICIENT_CANDLES:{len(df)}",
        )

    tail = df.tail(MIN_CANDLES)["ts"]

    diffs = tail.diff().dropna()

    if not (diffs == BAR).all():

        bad = diffs[diffs != BAR]

        sample = (
            str(bad.iloc[0])
            if len(bad)
            else "unknown"
        )

        return (
            False,
            f"GAP_OR_DUPLICATE:{sample}",
        )

    return True, "OK"


# ============================================================
# INDICATORS
# ============================================================

def prepare(df):

    d = df.copy()

    previous_close = d["close"].shift(1)

    tr = pd.concat(
        [
            d["high"] - d["low"],
            (
                d["high"]
                - previous_close
            ).abs(),
            (
                d["low"]
                - previous_close
            ).abs(),
        ],
        axis=1,
    ).max(axis=1)

    # Frozen Candidate 11 ATR.
    d["atr20"] = tr.ewm(
        alpha=1 / ATR_N,
        adjust=False,
    ).mean()

    # Frozen Candidate 11 Z-score.
    # pandas rolling std default ddof=1.
    mean20 = (
        d["close"]
        .rolling(ZSCORE_N)
        .mean()
    )

    std20 = (
        d["close"]
        .rolling(ZSCORE_N)
        .std()
    )

    d["z20"] = (
        d["close"] - mean20
    ) / std20.replace(
        0,
        np.nan,
    )

    # Frozen EMA200.
    d["ema200"] = (
        d["close"]
        .ewm(
            span=EMA_N,
            adjust=False,
        )
        .mean()
    )

    # Frozen RANGE20.
    d["range20"] = (
        d["high"]
        .rolling(RANGE_N)
        .max()
        -
        d["low"]
        .rolling(RANGE_N)
        .min()
    )

    # Median of RANGE20.
    d["range_median20"] = (
        d["range20"]
        .rolling(RANGE_N)
        .median()
    )

    # Median volume.
    d["vol_median20"] = (
        d["volume"]
        .rolling(VOL_N)
        .median()
    )

    return d


# ============================================================
# SIGNAL LOGIC
# ============================================================

def signal_reason(d, i):

    r = d.iloc[i]

    p = (
        d.iloc[i - 1]
        if i > 0
        else None
    )

    required = [
        "z20",
        "ema200",
        "range20",
        "range_median20",
        "vol_median20",
        "atr20",
    ]

    if any(
        not np.isfinite(float(r[x]))
        for x in required
    ):
        return "WARMUP_NAN"

    if (
        p is None
        or not np.isfinite(
            float(p["z20"])
        )
    ):
        return "NO_Z_CROSS"

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # FROZEN SHORT TRIGGER
    # Previous Z >= +2
    # Current Z < +2
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not (
        float(p["z20"]) >= 2.0
        and
        float(r["z20"]) < 2.0
    ):
        return "NO_Z_CROSS"

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # PRICE BELOW EMA200
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not (
        float(r["close"])
        < float(r["ema200"])
    ):
        return "ABOVE_EMA200"

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # RANGE20 >= RANGE MEDIAN20
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not (
        float(r["range20"])
        >= float(r["range_median20"])
    ):
        return "LOW_RANGE"

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # VOLUME >= VOLUME MEDIAN20
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not (
        float(r["volume"])
        >= float(r["vol_median20"])
    ):
        return "LOW_VOLUME"

    atr = float(r["atr20"])

    if not np.isfinite(atr) or atr <= 0:
        return "INVALID_ATR"

    return "SIGNAL"


# ============================================================
# DUPLICATE CONTROL
# ============================================================

def already_recorded(
    state,
    symbol,
    signal_ts,
):

    target = pd.Timestamp(
        signal_ts
    ).isoformat()

    for item in state["signals"]:

        if (
            item.get("symbol") == symbol
            and
            item.get("signal_ts") == target
        ):
            return True

    for item in state["missed"]:

        if (
            item.get("symbol") == symbol
            and
            item.get("signal_ts") == target
        ):
            return True

    for item in state["closed"]:

        if (
            item.get("symbol") == symbol
            and
            item.get("signal_ts") == target
        ):
            return True

    for item in state["open"].values():

        if (
            item.get("symbol") == symbol
            and
            item.get("signal_ts") == target
        ):
            return True

    return False


def add_missed(
    state,
    symbol,
    signal_ts,
    reason,
    extra=None,
):

    item = {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "signal_ts": iso(signal_ts),
        "entry_ts": iso(
            pd.Timestamp(signal_ts)
            + BAR
        ),
        "reason": reason,
    }

    if extra:
        item.update(extra)

    state["missed"].append(item)

    if len(state["missed"]) > MAX_MISSED:
        del state["missed"][
            :-MAX_MISSED
        ]


# ============================================================
# SIGNAL -> NEXT CANDLE OPEN
# ============================================================

def handle_signal(
    state,
    symbol,
    d,
    entry_source,
    i,
    now,
):

    signal_ts = d.iloc[i]["ts"]

    if already_recorded(
        state,
        symbol,
        signal_ts,
    ):
        return "DUPLICATE"

    entry_ts = (
        signal_ts + BAR
    )

    # Registration boundary: entry before eligibility is never evidence.
    if entry_ts < pd.Timestamp(
        state["eligible_from"]
    ):

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "BEFORE_REGISTRATION_START",
        )

        return "MISSED"

    delay_min = (
        pd.Timestamp(now)
        - entry_ts
    ).total_seconds() / 60.0

    # Signal's next candle has not opened yet.
    if delay_min < 0:
        return "NOT_DUE"

    # NEVER use signal candle close as fallback entry.
    if delay_min > MAX_ENTRY_DELAY_MIN:

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "ENTRY_DELAY_EXCEEDED",
            {
                "delay_min": round(
                    delay_min,
                    2,
                )
            },
        )

        return "MISSED"

    # Entry is exactly next candle OPEN.
    entry_row = entry_source[
        entry_source["ts"] == entry_ts
    ]

    if len(entry_row) != 1:

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "ENTRY_CANDLE_UNAVAILABLE",
            {
                "delay_min": round(
                    delay_min,
                    2,
                )
            },
        )

        return "MISSED"

    row = entry_row.iloc[0]

    entry = float(row["open"])

    atr = float(
        d.iloc[i]["atr20"]
    )

    if (
        not np.isfinite(entry)
        or entry <= 0
        or not np.isfinite(atr)
        or atr <= 0
    ):

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "INVALID_ENTRY_OR_ATR",
        )

        return "MISSED"

    risk = SL_ATR * atr

    if (
        not np.isfinite(risk)
        or risk <= 0
    ):

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "INVALID_RISK",
        )

        return "MISSED"

    # One live position per symbol.
    if symbol in [
        p.get("symbol")
        for p in state["open"].values()
    ]:

        add_missed(
            state,
            symbol,
            signal_ts,
            "SYMBOL_ALREADY_OPEN",
        )

        return "MISSED"

    pos_id = (
        f"{symbol}|"
        f"{iso(signal_ts)}"
    )

    position = {

        "id": pos_id,

        "symbol": symbol,

        "side": "SHORT",

        "signal_ts": iso(
            signal_ts
        ),

        "entry_ts": iso(
            entry_ts
        ),

        "entry": entry,

        "atr": atr,

        "risk": risk,

        "risk_pct": risk / entry,

        "sl": entry + risk,

        "tp": entry - TP_R * risk,

        "bars": 0,

        "last_bar_ts": None,

        "source": "next_candle_open",

        "status": "OPEN",
    }

    state["open"][pos_id] = position

    state["signals"].append(
        {
            "id": pos_id,

            "symbol": symbol,

            "signal_ts": iso(
                signal_ts
            ),

            "entry_ts": iso(
                entry_ts
            ),

            "entry": entry,

            "atr": atr,

            "risk": risk,

            "sl": entry + risk,

            "tp": entry - TP_R * risk,

            "source": "next_candle_open",
        }
    )

    if len(state["signals"]) > MAX_SIGNALS:
        del state["signals"][
            :-MAX_SIGNALS
        ]

    return "OPENED"


# ============================================================
# POSITION MONITORING
# ============================================================

def monitor_bar(
    state,
    symbol,
    row,
):

    ts = row["ts"]

    candidates = [
        (pid, p)
        for pid, p
        in state["open"].items()
        if p.get("symbol") == symbol
    ]

    for pos_id, pos in candidates:

        entry_ts = pd.Timestamp(
            pos["entry_ts"]
        )

        if ts < entry_ts:
            continue

        # Idempotency.
        if (
            pos.get("last_bar_ts")
            == iso(ts)
        ):
            continue

        pos["last_bar_ts"] = iso(ts)

        pos["bars"] = (
            int(pos.get("bars", 0))
            + 1
        )

        hi = float(row["high"])
        lo = float(row["low"])
        close = float(row["close"])

        entry = float(pos["entry"])
        risk = float(pos["risk"])

        sl = float(pos["sl"])
        tp = float(pos["tp"])

        # FROZEN INTRABAR PRIORITY:
        # SL FIRST.
        hit_sl = hi >= sl
        hit_tp = lo <= tp

        if hit_sl:

            gross_r = -1.0
            reason = "SL"

        elif hit_tp:

            gross_r = TP_R
            reason = "TP"

        elif pos["bars"] >= MAX_BARS:

            # Exit at close of the 30th monitored candle.
            gross_r = (
                entry - close
            ) / risk

            reason = "TIMEOUT"

        else:

            continue

        risk_pct = risk / entry

        net_r = (
            gross_r
            - (
                FEE_RT
                / risk_pct
            )
        )

        if reason == "SL":
            exit_price = sl
        elif reason == "TP":
            exit_price = tp
        else:
            exit_price = close

        # S3 decision scenario (registration section 6).
        bars_held = int(pos["bars"])

        s3_cost_fraction = (
            S3_FEE_PER_SIDE
            * (1.0 + exit_price / entry)
            + S3_FUNDING_PER_8H
            * bars_held
            * 0.5
        )

        net_r_s3 = (
            gross_r
            - s3_cost_fraction
            / risk_pct
        )

        stop_gap = bool(
            reason == "SL"
            and float(row["open"]) >= sl
        )

        closed = dict(pos)

        closed.update(
            {
                "status": "CLOSED",

                "exit_ts": iso(ts),

                "exit_price": exit_price,

                "exit_reason": reason,

                "gross_r": gross_r,

                "net_r": net_r,

                "net_r_s3": net_r_s3,

                "stop_gap": stop_gap,

                "bars_held": pos["bars"],

                "fee_assumption_rt": FEE_RT,
            }
        )

        state["closed"].append(
            closed
        )

        if len(state["closed"]) > MAX_CLOSED:
            del state["closed"][
                :-MAX_CLOSED
            ]

        del state["open"][pos_id]

        return reason

    return None


# ============================================================
# SYMBOL PROCESSING
# ============================================================

def process_symbol(
    state,
    symbol,
    raw,
    now,
):

    completed = completed_only(
        raw,
        now,
    )

    if completed.empty:

        state["symbols"][symbol][
            "last_status"
        ] = "NO_COMPLETED_DATA"

        raise RuntimeError(
            "NO_COMPLETED_DATA"
        )

    ok, status = check_contiguous(
        completed
    )

    # IMPORTANT:
    # Data-quality failure is a real symbol failure.
    # Never silently return OK.
    if not ok:

        state["symbols"][symbol][
            "last_status"
        ] = status

        raise RuntimeError(status)

    d = prepare(completed)

    st = state["symbols"][symbol]

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # FIRST RUN:
    # Do NOT replay historical signals.
    # Start from the latest completed candle.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if not st.get("baseline_done"):

        if len(d) < 2:

            st["last_status"] = (
                "BASELINE_WAIT"
            )

            return {
                "signals": 0,
                "opened": 0,
                "closed": 0,
                "missed": 0,
                "reasons": {},
            }

        st["last_ts"] = iso(
            d.iloc[-2]["ts"]
        )

        st["baseline_done"] = True

    last_ts = (
        pd.Timestamp(st["last_ts"])
        if st.get("last_ts")
        else None
    )

    if last_ts is not None:

        new_rows = d[
            d["ts"] > last_ts
        ].copy()

    else:

        new_rows = d.iloc[-1:].copy()

    stats = {
        "signals": 0,
        "opened": 0,
        "closed": 0,
        "missed": 0,
        "reasons": {},
    }

    for _, row in new_rows.iterrows():

        ts = row["ts"]

        idxs = d.index[
            d["ts"] == ts
        ]

        if len(idxs) != 1:
            continue

        idx = int(idxs[0])

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # FIRST: monitor existing position
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        close_reason = monitor_bar(
            state,
            symbol,
            row,
        )

        if close_reason:
            stats["closed"] += 1

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # SECOND: evaluate new signal
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        reason = signal_reason(
            d,
            idx,
        )

        stats["reasons"][reason] = (
            stats["reasons"].get(reason, 0)
            + 1
        )

        if reason == "SIGNAL":

            stats["signals"] += 1

            result = handle_signal(
                state,
                symbol,
                d,
                raw,
                idx,
                now,
            )

            if result == "OPENED":
                stats["opened"] += 1

            elif result == "MISSED":
                stats["missed"] += 1

        # ----------------------------------------------------
        # ADVANCE CURSOR
        # ----------------------------------------------------

        st["last_ts"] = iso(ts)

    st["last_status"] = "OK"

    return stats


# ============================================================
# REPORT
# ============================================================

def report(
    state,
    now,
    run_stats,
    ok_count,
    fail_count,
):

    closed = state["closed"]

    gross = [
        float(x["gross_r"])
        for x in closed
        if np.isfinite(
            float(
                x.get(
                    "gross_r",
                    np.nan,
                )
            )
        )
    ]

    net = [
        float(x["net_r"])
        for x in closed
        if np.isfinite(
            float(
                x.get(
                    "net_r",
                    np.nan,
                )
            )
        )
    ]

    def pf(values):

        gp = sum(
            x for x in values
            if x > 0
        )

        gl = -sum(
            x for x in values
            if x < 0
        )

        if gl <= 0:
            return float("inf")

        return gp / gl

    def max_dd(values):

        if not values:
            return 0.0

        eq = np.cumsum(values)

        peak = np.maximum.accumulate(
            eq
        )

        return float(
            np.min(eq - peak)
        )

    net_total = (
        float(sum(net))
        if net
        else 0.0
    )

    net_exp = (
        float(np.mean(net))
        if net
        else 0.0
    )

    net_pf = pf(net)

    wins = sum(
        x > 0
        for x in net
    )

    wr = (
        wins / len(net)
        if net
        else 0.0
    )

    by_reason = {}

    for x in closed:

        r = x.get(
            "exit_reason",
            "UNKNOWN",
        )

        by_reason[r] = (
            by_reason.get(r, 0)
            + 1
        )

    print("\n" + "=" * 72)

    print(
        "U67-S — "
        "FORWARD PAPER TRADING (v1)"
    )

    print(
        "4H | SHORT ONLY | "
        "NEXT OPEN | SL 1.25 ATR | "
        "TP 2R | HOLD 30"
    )

    print(
        f"RUN_TIME_UTC = {iso(now)}"
    )

    print(
        f"STATE_FILE = {STATE_FILE}"
    )

    print(
        f"STATE_VERSION = {STATE_VERSION}"
    )

    print(
        "PAPER ONLY — NO REAL ORDERS"
    )

    print("=" * 72)

    print(
        f"SYMBOLS_OK={ok_count}/{len(SYMS)} "
        f"FAILED={fail_count}"
    )

    print(
        f"THIS RUN: "
        f"NEW_SIGNALS="
        f"{run_stats['signals']} "
        f"OPENED="
        f"{run_stats['opened']} "
        f"CLOSED="
        f"{run_stats['closed']} "
        f"MISSED="
        f"{run_stats['missed']}"
    )

    # Registration section 7: no R values are reported before UC1.
    print(
        f"CLOSED={len(closed)} "
        f"| OPEN={len(state['open'])} "
        f"| SIGNALS={len(state['signals'])} "
        f"| MISSED={len(state['missed'])}"
    )

    print(
        f"ELIGIBLE_FROM={state['eligible_from']}"
    )

    print(
        f"EXIT_REASONS={by_reason}"
    )

    print(
        f"ASSUMED_ROUND_TRIP_COST="
        f"{FEE_RT * 100:.2f}% of price"
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SIGNAL REASONS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    reason_totals = {}

    for reason, count in run_stats[
        "reasons"
    ].items():

        reason_totals[reason] = (
            reason_totals.get(reason, 0)
            + count
        )

    if reason_totals:

        print(
            "THIS_RUN_SIGNAL_REASONS="
            f"{dict(sorted(reason_totals.items()))}"
        )

    if state["missed"]:

        print("RECENT_MISSED:")

        for x in state["missed"][-5:]:

            print(
                f"  {x['symbol']} "
                f"{x['signal_ts']} "
                f"-> {x['reason']}"
            )

    attempts = (
        len(state["signals"])
        + len(state["missed"])
    )

    missed_fraction = (
        len(state["missed"])
        / attempts
        if attempts
        else 0.0
    )

    print(
        f"MISSED_FRACTION="
        f"{missed_fraction:.3f}"
    )

    if (
        attempts
        and
        missed_fraction > MAX_FAIL_FRAC
    ):

        print(
            "WARNING: "
            "MISSED_FRACTION_ABOVE_LIMIT"
        )

    print("=" * 72)


# ============================================================
# MAIN
# ============================================================

def main():

    state, is_new = load_state()

    now = utc_now()

    # Snapshot before processing.
    # This lets us save ONLY if the state really changed.
    before_fingerprint = state_fingerprint(
        state
    )

    print(
        "U67-S — "
        "FORWARD PAPER TRADING ENGINE (v1)"
    )

    print(
        "4H | SHORT ONLY | "
        "NEXT CANDLE OPEN | "
        "SL 1.25 ATR | TP 2R | HOLD 30"
    )

    print(
        f"RUN_TIME_UTC = {now.isoformat()}"
    )

    print(
        f"STATE = "
        f"{'NEW' if is_new else 'EXISTING'} "
        f"| open={len(state['open'])} "
        f"closed={len(state['closed'])}"
    )

    ok_count = 0
    fail_count = 0

    all_run_stats = {
        "signals": 0,
        "opened": 0,
        "closed": 0,
        "missed": 0,
        "reasons": {},
    }

    diagnostics = {}

    for symbol in SYMS:

        try:

            raw = fetch_raw(
                symbol,
                now,
            )

            completed = completed_only(
                raw,
                now,
            )

            diagnostics[symbol] = {
                "raw": len(raw),
                "completed": len(
                    completed
                ),
                "last_completed": (
                    iso(
                        completed[
                            "ts"
                        ].iloc[-1]
                    )
                    if not completed.empty
                    else None
                ),
            }

            if len(completed) < MIN_CANDLES:

                raise RuntimeError(
                    "INSUFFICIENT_COMPLETED_CANDLES:"
                    f"{len(completed)}"
                )

            stats = process_symbol(
                state,
                symbol,
                raw,
                now,
            )

            ok_count += 1

            for key in [
                "signals",
                "opened",
                "closed",
                "missed",
            ]:

                all_run_stats[key] += (
                    stats[key]
                )

            for reason, count in stats[
                "reasons"
            ].items():

                all_run_stats[
                    "reasons"
                ][reason] = (
                    all_run_stats[
                        "reasons"
                    ].get(reason, 0)
                    + count
                )

        except Exception as e:

            fail_count += 1

            diagnostics[symbol] = {
                "error": str(e)
            }

            state["errors"].append(
                {
                    "ts": now.isoformat(),
                    "symbol": symbol,
                    "error": str(e),
                }
            )

            if len(state["errors"]) > MAX_ERRORS:
                del state["errors"][
                    :-MAX_ERRORS
                ]

            state["symbols"][symbol][
                "last_status"
            ] = (
                "ERROR:"
                + str(e)[:160]
            )

            print(
                f"FETCH/PROCESS ERROR "
                f"{symbol}: "
                f"{str(e)[:180]}"
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # DATA QUALITY SUMMARY
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    valid_last = [
        x["last_completed"]
        for x in diagnostics.values()
        if x.get("last_completed")
    ]

    if valid_last:

        print(
            "LAST CLOSED CANDLE = "
            f"{max(valid_last)}"
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # FAILURE THRESHOLD
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    failure_fraction = (
        fail_count / len(SYMS)
        if SYMS
        else 1.0
    )

    if (
        failure_fraction
        > MAX_FAIL_FRAC
    ):

        print(
            "RUN_STATUS = "
            "DEGRADED / "
            "TOO_MANY_SYMBOL_FAILURES"
        )

        # Save diagnostic/error state only if changed.
        after_fingerprint = state_fingerprint(
            state
        )

        if (
            is_new
            or
            after_fingerprint
            != before_fingerprint
        ):
            atomic_save(state)
            print(
                "STATE_SAVE = CHANGED"
            )
        else:
            print(
                "STATE_SAVE = UNCHANGED"
            )

        sys.exit(2)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # STATE SAVE
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    after_fingerprint = state_fingerprint(
        state
    )

    if (
        is_new
        or
        after_fingerprint
        != before_fingerprint
    ):

        atomic_save(state)

        print(
            "STATE_SAVE = CHANGED"
        )

    else:

        print(
            "STATE_SAVE = UNCHANGED"
        )

    report(
        state,
        now,
        all_run_stats,
        ok_count,
        fail_count,
    )

    print(
        "RUN_STATUS = OK"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN FILE u67_audit/evaluators/evaluate_u67.py sha256=f3cc549266fdedcadaa1cab4e71b3ef4a58d9639c87172c4700a3f7bbee7f081 bytes=15097 =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# U67-S EVALUATOR v1 (read-only).
# Implements U67_S_REGISTRATION_v1.txt sections 0e, 6, 7, 8 and the UNRESOLVED rule.
# Registration sha256: 9efe88d1b07c166be1e4e371e3c2339fc941194b22f8cf03fd5c8d8fe879d8be
#
# Properties:
#  - Never modifies the repository or any state file (git show / git log only).
#  - Phase 1 "scan": prints COUNTS ONLY (no R value anywhere).
#  - Phase 2 "evaluate": refuses to run unless the trigger holds in git history;
#    always evaluates the FIRST committed state that satisfies the trigger,
#    never HEAD. Evaluation instant = that commit's committer time.
#  - Deterministic: no clock reads, fixed seed, fixed ordering.
#
# Implementation choices NOT spelled out in the registration (declared here,
# covered by this script's sha256, changeable only under registration section 9):
#  1. Bootstrap: for each scenario a fresh numpy default_rng(20261028) draws
#     rng.integers(0, n, size=(20000, n)); statistic = mean over the drawn
#     cluster means. Identical resample indices across scenarios (paired).
#  2. "Commit time" = git committer date (%cI) of the state commit.
#  3. Cluster complete = commit_time >= entry_ts + 90 minutes.
#  4. Max concurrent open trades: a trade occupies [entry_ts, exit_ts + 4h)
#     (UNRESOLVED: [entry_ts, entry_ts + 30 x 4h)); counted at interval starts.
#  5. Adjacent-merge sensitivity: strict clusters whose signal_ts differ by
#     exactly 4h are chained; merged outcome = equal-weight mean of all trades
#     of the chained clusters; statistic = mean of merged outcomes.
#  6. Profit factor and drawdown use the S3 net R of trades.
#  7. BTC change is only printed if --btc-start and --btc-end are supplied.
#  8. Trigger detection (scan, and the trigger check inside evaluate) is COUNT-ONLY:
#     it builds trades with with_r=False, so no gross/net R or cost is computed before
#     the trigger holds. The state JSON is parsed whole (it contains R fields as plain
#     data) but none of them is read by the scan path.
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

STATE_FILE_DEFAULT = "u67_short_paper_state.json"
REG_ELIGIBLE = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-28T12:00:00Z")
SEED = 20261028
DRAWS = 20000
UNRESOLVED_DAYS = 45
MAX_ENTRY_DELAY_MIN = 90
BAR = pd.Timedelta(hours=4)
SCEN = ["S0", "S3", "S3a", "S3b", "S1", "S2"]
CHECKPOINT_N = {"UC1": 15, "UC2": 30}
CAP_DAYS = {"UC1": 120, "UC2": 240}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- git helpers
def git(repo, *args):
    p = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo] + list(args), capture_output=True, text=True)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("git %s failed: %s" % (" ".join(args), p.stderr.strip()[:300]))
    return p.stdout


def state_commits(repo, state_file):
    out = git(repo, "log", "--first-parent", "--reverse", "--format=%H%x09%cI", "--", state_file)
    rows = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            h, t = line.split("\t")
            rows.append((h, pd.Timestamp(t).tz_convert("UTC")))
    return rows


def load_state_at(repo, commit, state_file):
    try:
        return json.loads(git(repo, "show", "%s:%s" % (commit, state_file)))
    except RuntimeError:
        return None  # file absent at that commit (deletion) -> defect label by caller


# ------------------------------------------------------------ trade building
def ts(x):
    return pd.Timestamp(x).tz_convert("UTC") if pd.Timestamp(x).tzinfo else pd.Timestamp(x, tz="UTC")


def cost_fraction(scn, entry, exit_price, bars, reason):
    ratio = 1.0 + exit_price / entry
    if scn == "S0":
        return 0.002
    s3 = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.00010 * bars * 0.5
    if scn == "S3":
        return s3
    if scn == "S3a":
        return s3 + 0.00025 * ratio
    if scn == "S3b":
        return s3 + 0.0005 * ratio
    if scn == "S1":
        return 0.0006 * ratio
    if scn == "S2":
        return (0.0004 if reason == "TP" else 0.0006) * ratio
    raise ValueError(scn)


def build_trades(state, commit_time, with_r=True):
    """Return list of trade dicts (closed + UNRESOLVED) and list of still-open trades
    that are NOT unresolved. Only eligible trades (entry_ts >= later of constant,
    state eligible_from)."""
    elig = max(REG_ELIGIBLE, ts(state["eligible_from"]))
    syms = state["symbols"]
    trades, open_pending = [], []
    seen = set()
    for c in state["closed"]:
        if c["id"] in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(c["id"])
        if ts(c["entry_ts"]) < elig:
            continue
        t = dict(id=c["id"], symbol=c["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(c["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(c["entry_ts"]),
                 exit_ts=ts(c["exit_ts"]), unresolved=False)
        if with_r:
            entry = float(c["entry"]); risk_pct = float(c["risk_pct"])
            bars = int(c["bars_held"]); xp = float(c["exit_price"]); reason = c["exit_reason"]
            gross = float(c["gross_r"])
            t.update(reason=reason, bars=bars, gross=gross)
            for s in SCEN:
                t[s] = gross - cost_fraction(s, entry, xp, bars, reason) / risk_pct
        trades.append(t)
    for pid, o in state["open"].items():
        oid = o.get("id", pid)
        if oid in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(oid)
        if ts(o["entry_ts"]) < elig:
            continue
        last_ts = syms.get(o["symbol"], {}).get("last_ts")
        age_ok = last_ts is not None and (commit_time - ts(last_ts)) >= pd.Timedelta(days=UNRESOLVED_DAYS)
        if not age_ok:
            open_pending.append(dict(id=oid, symbol=o["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(o["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"])))
            continue
        t = dict(id=oid, symbol=o["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(o["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"]),
                 exit_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"]) + 29 * BAR, unresolved=True)
        if with_r:
            entry = float(o["entry"]); risk_pct = float(o["risk_pct"]); sl = float(o["sl"])
            t.update(reason="SL", bars=30, gross=-1.0)
            for s in SCEN:
                t[s] = -1.0 - cost_fraction(s, entry, sl, 30, "SL") / risk_pct
        trades.append(t)
    return trades, open_pending


def first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n):
    """Strict clusters ordered by signal_ts, over OPENED eligible trades (closed, unresolved, still open)."""
    by = {}
    for t in trades:
        by.setdefault(t["signal_ts"], []).append(t)
    for p in pending:
        by.setdefault(p["signal_ts"], [])
    keys = sorted(by)
    if len(keys) < n:
        return None
    return keys[:n], by


def trigger_holds(state, commit_time, n):
    """Counts-only trigger. Returns (bool, info dict of counts)."""
    # COUNT-ONLY: with_r=False, so no net R / cost value is computed here.
    trades, pending = build_trades(state, commit_time, with_r=False)
    info = dict(trades=len(trades), pending_open=len(pending), clusters=0)
    keys_by = first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n)
    allk = sorted({t["signal_ts"] for t in trades} | {p["signal_ts"] for p in pending})
    info["clusters"] = len(allk)
    if keys_by is None:
        return False, info
    keys, by = keys_by
    pend_ts = {p["signal_ts"] for p in pending}
    for k in keys:
        entry_ts = k + BAR
        if commit_time < entry_ts + pd.Timedelta(minutes=MAX_ENTRY_DELAY_MIN):
            return False, info
        if k in pend_ts:
            return False, info
    return True, info


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ statistics
def cluster_outcomes(trades, keys, scn):
    by = {}
    for t in trades:
        if t["signal_ts"] in keys:
            by.setdefault(t["signal_ts"], []).append(t[scn])
    ks = sorted(by)
    return ks, np.array([float(np.mean(by[k])) for k in ks])


def boot_ci(means):
    n = len(means)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=(DRAWS, n))
    m = means[idx].mean(axis=1)
    return float(np.percentile(m, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(m, 97.5)), float((m > 0).mean())


def merged_adjacent(trades, keys, scn):
    ks = sorted(keys)
    groups, cur = [], [ks[0]]
    for a, b in zip(ks, ks[1:]):
        if b - a == BAR:
            cur.append(b)
        else:
            groups.append(cur); cur = [b]
    groups.append(cur)
    outs = []
    for g in groups:
        vals = [t[scn] for t in trades if t["signal_ts"] in g]
        outs.append(float(np.mean(vals)))
    return len(groups), float(np.mean(outs))


def max_dd(vals_in_order):
    peak = 0.0; cum = 0.0; dd = 0.0
    for v in vals_in_order:
        cum += v; peak = max(peak, cum); dd = min(dd, cum - peak)
    return dd


def max_concurrent(trades):
    ev = []
    for t in trades:
        end = t["entry_ts"] + 30 * BAR if t["unresolved"] else t["exit_ts"] + BAR
        ev.append((t["entry_ts"], end))
    best = 0
    for s, _ in ev:
        best = max(best, sum(1 for a, b in ev if a <= s < b))
    return best


def evaluate(state, commit, commit_time, first_created_at, cp, args):
    n = CHECKPOINT_N[cp]
    trades, pending = build_trades(state, commit_time)
    keys, _ = first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n)
    keyset = set(keys)
    sel = [t for t in trades if t["signal_ts"] in keyset]
    res = dict(checkpoint=cp, n_clusters=n, commit=commit, evaluation_instant=commit_time.isoformat(),
               created_at_state=state["created_at"], created_at_first_commit=first_created_at,
               created_at_check="OK" if state["created_at"] == first_created_at else "DEFECT_MISMATCH_LABEL_PERIOD",
               eligible_from=state["eligible_from"], n_trades=len(sel),
               n_unresolved=sum(1 for t in sel if t["unresolved"]),
               open_outside_selected_clusters=len([p for p in pending if p["signal_ts"] not in keyset]))
    res["cluster_keys"] = [k.isoformat() for k in keys]
    stats = {}
    for s in SCEN:
        ks, means = cluster_outcomes(sel, keyset, s)
        lo, hi, pgt = boot_ci(means)
        stats[s] = dict(cluster_mean=float(means.mean()), ci95_lo=lo, ci95_hi=hi, p_mean_gt0=pgt,
                        trade_mean=float(np.mean([t[s] for t in sel])),
                        merged_n=merged_adjacent(sel, keyset, s)[0], merged_mean=merged_adjacent(sel, keyset, s)[1])
    res["stats"] = stats
    # S3 jackknife
    ks, m3 = cluster_outcomes(sel, keyset, "S3")
    order = sorted(range(len(ks)), key=lambda i: (-m3[i], ks[i]))
    big = order[0]
    rem = [m3[i] for i in range(len(ks)) if i != big]
    jk_cluster = float(np.mean(rem)) if rem else None
    month = {}
    for k, v in zip(ks, m3):
        month.setdefault(k.strftime("%Y-%m"), []).append(v)
    best_m = sorted(month, key=lambda mth: (-sum(month[mth]), mth))[0]
    remm = [v for k, v in zip(ks, m3) if k.strftime("%Y-%m") != best_m]
    jk_month = float(np.mean(remm)) if remm else None
    res["jackknife_S3"] = dict(largest_cluster=ks[big].isoformat(), mean_without_largest=jk_cluster,
                               best_month=best_m, mean_without_best_month=jk_month)
    # UNRESOLVED exclusion sensitivity (S3)
    nu = [t for t in sel if not t["unresolved"]]
    if nu and res["n_unresolved"]:
        ks2 = {t["signal_ts"] for t in nu}
        _, m2 = cluster_outcomes(nu, ks2, "S3")
        res["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] = float(m2.mean())
    else:
        res["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] = res["stats"]["S3"]["cluster_mean"]
    # descriptive
    ordered = sorted(sel, key=lambda t: (t["exit_ts"], t["signal_ts"], t["symbol"]))
    v = [t["S3"] for t in ordered]
    wins = sum(x for x in v if x > 0); losses = -sum(x for x in v if x < 0)
    res["profit_factor_S3"] = "n/a" if losses == 0 else wins / losses
    res["max_drawdown_R_S3"] = max_dd(v)
    res["max_concurrent_open"] = max_concurrent(sel)
    res["by_month_S3"] = {m: dict(n_clusters=len(x), cluster_mean=float(np.mean(x))) for m, x in sorted(month.items())}
    bysym = {}
    for t in sel:
        bysym.setdefault(t["symbol"], []).append(t["S3"])
    res["by_symbol_S3"] = {s: dict(n=len(x), mean=float(np.mean(x))) for s, x in sorted(bysym.items())}
    if args.btc_start and args.btc_end:
        res["btc_change_pct"] = (args.btc_end / args.btc_start - 1) * 100
    else:
        res["btc_change_pct"] = "NOT PROVIDED"
    # decision
    s3 = stats["S3"]
    if cp == "UC1":
        res["verdict"] = "REJECTED" if s3["cluster_mean"] <= 0 else "CONTINUE_TO_UC2 (no verdict; CI and jackknife descriptive)"
    else:
        if s3["cluster_mean"] <= 0:
            res["verdict"] = "REJECTED"
        else:
            def pos(x):
                return x is not None and x > 0
            if s3["ci95_lo"] > 0 and pos(jk_cluster) and pos(jk_month):
                res["verdict"] = "PAPER GATE PASSED"
            else:
                res["verdict"] = "NOT ESTABLISHED (final for U67-S v1)"
    return res


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ main
def scan(repo, state_file, cp):
    n = CHECKPOINT_N[cp]
    commits = state_commits(repo, state_file)
    if not commits:
        return None, dict(commits=0), None
    first_state = load_state_at(repo, commits[0][0], state_file)
    first_created = first_state["created_at"] if first_state else None
    missing = []
    last_info = {}
    for h, t in commits:
        st = load_state_at(repo, h, state_file)
        if st is None:
            missing.append(h)
            continue
        ok, info = trigger_holds(st, t, n)
        last_info = dict(commit=h, commit_time=t.isoformat(), **info)
        if ok:
            return (h, t, st, first_created), dict(commits=len(commits), missing_state_commits=missing, **last_info), first_created
    return None, dict(commits=len(commits), missing_state_commits=missing, **last_info), first_created


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--repo", default=".")
    ap.add_argument("--state", default=STATE_FILE_DEFAULT)
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint", choices=["UC1", "UC2"], required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=["scan", "evaluate"], default="scan")
    ap.add_argument("--btc-start", type=float)
    ap.add_argument("--btc-end", type=float)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    hit, info, first_created = scan(a.repo, a.state, a.checkpoint)
    if a.mode == "scan" or hit is None:
        # COUNTS ONLY
        out = dict(checkpoint=a.checkpoint, trigger_holds=hit is not None, counts=info)
        if hit is not None:
            out["first_satisfying_commit"] = hit[0]
            out["instant"] = hit[1].isoformat()
        print(json.dumps(out, sort_keys=True, indent=1))
        if a.mode == "evaluate" and hit is None:
            print("REFUSED: trigger does not hold; no R value is computed or shown.", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(3)
        return
    h, t, st, fc = hit
    res = evaluate(st, h, t, fc, a.checkpoint, a)
    txt = json.dumps(res, sort_keys=True, indent=1, default=str)
    print(txt)
    print("RESULT_SHA256 " + hashlib.sha256(txt.encode()).hexdigest())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
===== END FILE u67_audit/evaluators/evaluate_u67.py =====

===== BEGIN FILE u67_audit/evaluators/evaluate_u67_long.py sha256=5b2c5571692971b14fd8074b3d7f504a63073e442b53d7687045af2fa6e0f767 bytes=15239 =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# U67-L EVALUATOR v1 (read-only). Direction-neutral copy of the U67-S evaluator:
# costs depend only on exit/entry ratio, bars and stored stop; UNRESOLVED uses the stored stop.
# Implements U67_L_REGISTRATION_v1.txt sections 0e, 6, 7, 8 and the UNRESOLVED rule.
# Registration sha256: 3f0298f2314d1dc54c7768dbe181b41839e5223f9903356b7a1b903f2af5b02a
#
# Properties:
#  - Never modifies the repository or any state file (git show / git log only).
#  - Phase 1 "scan": prints COUNTS ONLY (no R value anywhere).
#  - Phase 2 "evaluate": refuses to run unless the trigger holds in git history;
#    always evaluates the FIRST committed state that satisfies the trigger,
#    never HEAD. Evaluation instant = that commit's committer time.
#  - Deterministic: no clock reads, fixed seed, fixed ordering.
#
# Implementation choices NOT spelled out in the registration (declared here,
# covered by this script's sha256, changeable only under registration section 9):
#  1. Bootstrap: for each scenario a fresh numpy default_rng(20261029) draws
#     rng.integers(0, n, size=(20000, n)); statistic = mean over the drawn
#     cluster means. Identical resample indices across scenarios (paired).
#  2. "Commit time" = git committer date (%cI) of the state commit.
#  3. Cluster complete = commit_time >= entry_ts + 90 minutes.
#  4. Max concurrent open trades: a trade occupies [entry_ts, exit_ts + 4h)
#     (UNRESOLVED: [entry_ts, entry_ts + 30 x 4h)); counted at interval starts.
#  5. Adjacent-merge sensitivity: strict clusters whose signal_ts differ by
#     exactly 4h are chained; merged outcome = equal-weight mean of all trades
#     of the chained clusters; statistic = mean of merged outcomes.
#  6. Profit factor and drawdown use the S3 net R of trades.
#  7. BTC change is only printed if --btc-start and --btc-end are supplied.
#  8. Trigger detection (scan, and the trigger check inside evaluate) is COUNT-ONLY:
#     it builds trades with with_r=False, so no gross/net R or cost is computed before
#     the trigger holds. The state JSON is parsed whole (it contains R fields as plain
#     data) but none of them is read by the scan path.
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

STATE_FILE_DEFAULT = "u67_long_paper_state.json"
REG_ELIGIBLE = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-30T12:00:00Z")
SEED = 20261029
DRAWS = 20000
UNRESOLVED_DAYS = 45
MAX_ENTRY_DELAY_MIN = 90
BAR = pd.Timedelta(hours=4)
SCEN = ["S0", "S3", "S3a", "S3b", "S1", "S2"]
CHECKPOINT_N = {"UL1": 15, "UL2": 30}
CAP_DAYS = {"UL1": 120, "UL2": 240}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- git helpers
def git(repo, *args):
    p = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo] + list(args), capture_output=True, text=True)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("git %s failed: %s" % (" ".join(args), p.stderr.strip()[:300]))
    return p.stdout


def state_commits(repo, state_file):
    out = git(repo, "log", "--first-parent", "--reverse", "--format=%H%x09%cI", "--", state_file)
    rows = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            h, t = line.split("\t")
            rows.append((h, pd.Timestamp(t).tz_convert("UTC")))
    return rows


def load_state_at(repo, commit, state_file):
    try:
        return json.loads(git(repo, "show", "%s:%s" % (commit, state_file)))
    except RuntimeError:
        return None  # file absent at that commit (deletion) -> defect label by caller


# ------------------------------------------------------------ trade building
def ts(x):
    return pd.Timestamp(x).tz_convert("UTC") if pd.Timestamp(x).tzinfo else pd.Timestamp(x, tz="UTC")


def cost_fraction(scn, entry, exit_price, bars, reason):
    ratio = 1.0 + exit_price / entry
    if scn == "S0":
        return 0.002
    s3 = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.00010 * bars * 0.5
    if scn == "S3":
        return s3
    if scn == "S3a":
        return s3 + 0.00025 * ratio
    if scn == "S3b":
        return s3 + 0.0005 * ratio
    if scn == "S1":
        return 0.0006 * ratio
    if scn == "S2":
        return (0.0004 if reason == "TP" else 0.0006) * ratio
    raise ValueError(scn)


def build_trades(state, commit_time, with_r=True):
    """Return list of trade dicts (closed + UNRESOLVED) and list of still-open trades
    that are NOT unresolved. Only eligible trades (entry_ts >= later of constant,
    state eligible_from)."""
    elig = max(REG_ELIGIBLE, ts(state["eligible_from"]))
    syms = state["symbols"]
    trades, open_pending = [], []
    seen = set()
    for c in state["closed"]:
        if c["id"] in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(c["id"])
        if ts(c["entry_ts"]) < elig:
            continue
        t = dict(id=c["id"], symbol=c["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(c["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(c["entry_ts"]),
                 exit_ts=ts(c["exit_ts"]), unresolved=False)
        if with_r:
            entry = float(c["entry"]); risk_pct = float(c["risk_pct"])
            bars = int(c["bars_held"]); xp = float(c["exit_price"]); reason = c["exit_reason"]
            gross = float(c["gross_r"])
            t.update(reason=reason, bars=bars, gross=gross)
            for s in SCEN:
                t[s] = gross - cost_fraction(s, entry, xp, bars, reason) / risk_pct
        trades.append(t)
    for pid, o in state["open"].items():
        oid = o.get("id", pid)
        if oid in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(oid)
        if ts(o["entry_ts"]) < elig:
            continue
        last_ts = syms.get(o["symbol"], {}).get("last_ts")
        age_ok = last_ts is not None and (commit_time - ts(last_ts)) >= pd.Timedelta(days=UNRESOLVED_DAYS)
        if not age_ok:
            open_pending.append(dict(id=oid, symbol=o["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(o["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"])))
            continue
        t = dict(id=oid, symbol=o["symbol"], signal_ts=ts(o["signal_ts"]), entry_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"]),
                 exit_ts=ts(o["entry_ts"]) + 29 * BAR, unresolved=True)
        if with_r:
            entry = float(o["entry"]); risk_pct = float(o["risk_pct"]); sl = float(o["sl"])
            t.update(reason="SL", bars=30, gross=-1.0)
            for s in SCEN:
                t[s] = -1.0 - cost_fraction(s, entry, sl, 30, "SL") / risk_pct
        trades.append(t)
    return trades, open_pending


def first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n):
    """Strict clusters ordered by signal_ts, over OPENED eligible trades (closed, unresolved, still open)."""
    by = {}
    for t in trades:
        by.setdefault(t["signal_ts"], []).append(t)
    for p in pending:
        by.setdefault(p["signal_ts"], [])
    keys = sorted(by)
    if len(keys) < n:
        return None
    return keys[:n], by


def trigger_holds(state, commit_time, n):
    """Counts-only trigger. Returns (bool, info dict of counts)."""
    # COUNT-ONLY: with_r=False, so no net R / cost value is computed here.
    trades, pending = build_trades(state, commit_time, with_r=False)
    info = dict(trades=len(trades), pending_open=len(pending), clusters=0)
    keys_by = first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n)
    allk = sorted({t["signal_ts"] for t in trades} | {p["signal_ts"] for p in pending})
    info["clusters"] = len(allk)
    if keys_by is None:
        return False, info
    keys, by = keys_by
    pend_ts = {p["signal_ts"] for p in pending}
    for k in keys:
        entry_ts = k + BAR
        if commit_time < entry_ts + pd.Timedelta(minutes=MAX_ENTRY_DELAY_MIN):
            return False, info
        if k in pend_ts:
            return False, info
    return True, info


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ statistics
def cluster_outcomes(trades, keys, scn):
    by = {}
    for t in trades:
        if t["signal_ts"] in keys:
            by.setdefault(t["signal_ts"], []).append(t[scn])
    ks = sorted(by)
    return ks, np.array([float(np.mean(by[k])) for k in ks])


def boot_ci(means):
    n = len(means)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=(DRAWS, n))
    m = means[idx].mean(axis=1)
    return float(np.percentile(m, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(m, 97.5)), float((m > 0).mean())


def merged_adjacent(trades, keys, scn):
    ks = sorted(keys)
    groups, cur = [], [ks[0]]
    for a, b in zip(ks, ks[1:]):
        if b - a == BAR:
            cur.append(b)
        else:
            groups.append(cur); cur = [b]
    groups.append(cur)
    outs = []
    for g in groups:
        vals = [t[scn] for t in trades if t["signal_ts"] in g]
        outs.append(float(np.mean(vals)))
    return len(groups), float(np.mean(outs))


def max_dd(vals_in_order):
    peak = 0.0; cum = 0.0; dd = 0.0
    for v in vals_in_order:
        cum += v; peak = max(peak, cum); dd = min(dd, cum - peak)
    return dd


def max_concurrent(trades):
    ev = []
    for t in trades:
        end = t["entry_ts"] + 30 * BAR if t["unresolved"] else t["exit_ts"] + BAR
        ev.append((t["entry_ts"], end))
    best = 0
    for s, _ in ev:
        best = max(best, sum(1 for a, b in ev if a <= s < b))
    return best


def evaluate(state, commit, commit_time, first_created_at, cp, args):
    n = CHECKPOINT_N[cp]
    trades, pending = build_trades(state, commit_time)
    keys, _ = first_n_clusters(trades, pending, n)
    keyset = set(keys)
    sel = [t for t in trades if t["signal_ts"] in keyset]
    res = dict(checkpoint=cp, n_clusters=n, commit=commit, evaluation_instant=commit_time.isoformat(),
               created_at_state=state["created_at"], created_at_first_commit=first_created_at,
               created_at_check="OK" if state["created_at"] == first_created_at else "DEFECT_MISMATCH_LABEL_PERIOD",
               eligible_from=state["eligible_from"], n_trades=len(sel),
               n_unresolved=sum(1 for t in sel if t["unresolved"]),
               open_outside_selected_clusters=len([p for p in pending if p["signal_ts"] not in keyset]))
    res["cluster_keys"] = [k.isoformat() for k in keys]
    stats = {}
    for s in SCEN:
        ks, means = cluster_outcomes(sel, keyset, s)
        lo, hi, pgt = boot_ci(means)
        stats[s] = dict(cluster_mean=float(means.mean()), ci95_lo=lo, ci95_hi=hi, p_mean_gt0=pgt,
                        trade_mean=float(np.mean([t[s] for t in sel])),
                        merged_n=merged_adjacent(sel, keyset, s)[0], merged_mean=merged_adjacent(sel, keyset, s)[1])
    res["stats"] = stats
    # S3 jackknife
    ks, m3 = cluster_outcomes(sel, keyset, "S3")
    order = sorted(range(len(ks)), key=lambda i: (-m3[i], ks[i]))
    big = order[0]
    rem = [m3[i] for i in range(len(ks)) if i != big]
    jk_cluster = float(np.mean(rem)) if rem else None
    month = {}
    for k, v in zip(ks, m3):
        month.setdefault(k.strftime("%Y-%m"), []).append(v)
    best_m = sorted(month, key=lambda mth: (-sum(month[mth]), mth))[0]
    remm = [v for k, v in zip(ks, m3) if k.strftime("%Y-%m") != best_m]
    jk_month = float(np.mean(remm)) if remm else None
    res["jackknife_S3"] = dict(largest_cluster=ks[big].isoformat(), mean_without_largest=jk_cluster,
                               best_month=best_m, mean_without_best_month=jk_month)
    # UNRESOLVED exclusion sensitivity (S3)
    nu = [t for t in sel if not t["unresolved"]]
    if nu and res["n_unresolved"]:
        ks2 = {t["signal_ts"] for t in nu}
        _, m2 = cluster_outcomes(nu, ks2, "S3")
        res["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] = float(m2.mean())
    else:
        res["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] = res["stats"]["S3"]["cluster_mean"]
    # descriptive
    ordered = sorted(sel, key=lambda t: (t["exit_ts"], t["signal_ts"], t["symbol"]))
    v = [t["S3"] for t in ordered]
    wins = sum(x for x in v if x > 0); losses = -sum(x for x in v if x < 0)
    res["profit_factor_S3"] = "n/a" if losses == 0 else wins / losses
    res["max_drawdown_R_S3"] = max_dd(v)
    res["max_concurrent_open"] = max_concurrent(sel)
    res["by_month_S3"] = {m: dict(n_clusters=len(x), cluster_mean=float(np.mean(x))) for m, x in sorted(month.items())}
    bysym = {}
    for t in sel:
        bysym.setdefault(t["symbol"], []).append(t["S3"])
    res["by_symbol_S3"] = {s: dict(n=len(x), mean=float(np.mean(x))) for s, x in sorted(bysym.items())}
    if args.btc_start and args.btc_end:
        res["btc_change_pct"] = (args.btc_end / args.btc_start - 1) * 100
    else:
        res["btc_change_pct"] = "NOT PROVIDED"
    # decision
    s3 = stats["S3"]
    if cp == "UL1":
        res["verdict"] = "REJECTED" if s3["cluster_mean"] <= 0 else "CONTINUE_TO_UL2 (no verdict; CI and jackknife descriptive)"
    else:
        if s3["cluster_mean"] <= 0:
            res["verdict"] = "REJECTED"
        else:
            def pos(x):
                return x is not None and x > 0
            if s3["ci95_lo"] > 0 and pos(jk_cluster) and pos(jk_month):
                res["verdict"] = "PAPER GATE PASSED"
            else:
                res["verdict"] = "NOT ESTABLISHED (final for U67-L v1)"
    return res


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ main
def scan(repo, state_file, cp):
    n = CHECKPOINT_N[cp]
    commits = state_commits(repo, state_file)
    if not commits:
        return None, dict(commits=0), None
    first_state = load_state_at(repo, commits[0][0], state_file)
    first_created = first_state["created_at"] if first_state else None
    missing = []
    last_info = {}
    for h, t in commits:
        st = load_state_at(repo, h, state_file)
        if st is None:
            missing.append(h)
            continue
        ok, info = trigger_holds(st, t, n)
        last_info = dict(commit=h, commit_time=t.isoformat(), **info)
        if ok:
            return (h, t, st, first_created), dict(commits=len(commits), missing_state_commits=missing, **last_info), first_created
    return None, dict(commits=len(commits), missing_state_commits=missing, **last_info), first_created


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--repo", default=".")
    ap.add_argument("--state", default=STATE_FILE_DEFAULT)
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint", choices=["UL1", "UL2"], required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=["scan", "evaluate"], default="scan")
    ap.add_argument("--btc-start", type=float)
    ap.add_argument("--btc-end", type=float)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    hit, info, first_created = scan(a.repo, a.state, a.checkpoint)
    if a.mode == "scan" or hit is None:
        # COUNTS ONLY
        out = dict(checkpoint=a.checkpoint, trigger_holds=hit is not None, counts=info)
        if hit is not None:
            out["first_satisfying_commit"] = hit[0]
            out["instant"] = hit[1].isoformat()
        print(json.dumps(out, sort_keys=True, indent=1))
        if a.mode == "evaluate" and hit is None:
            print("REFUSED: trigger does not hold; no R value is computed or shown.", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(3)
        return
    h, t, st, fc = hit
    res = evaluate(st, h, t, fc, a.checkpoint, a)
    txt = json.dumps(res, sort_keys=True, indent=1, default=str)
    print(txt)
    print("RESULT_SHA256 " + hashlib.sha256(txt.encode()).hexdigest())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

          spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("u67", "crypto_signal_bot_u67_short_v1.py")
          m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
          log = []
          orig = m.SESSION.get
          def wrapped(*a, **k):
              t = time.time()

                  log.append((r.status_code, time.time() - t, dict((h, r.headers.get(h)) for h in ("gw-ratelimit-limit","gw-ratelimit-remaining","gw-ratelimit-reset","retry-after"))))
                  return r
              except Exception as e:
                  log.append((type(e).__name__, time.time() - t, {}))
                  raise
          m.SESSION.get = wrapped
          sf = os.environ["U67_STATE_FILE"]
          def snap():
              s = json.load(open(sf))
              syms = s["symbols"]
              return dict(created_at=s["created_at"], eligible_from=s["eligible_from"],
                          n_syms=len(syms), baseline=sum(1 for v in syms.values() if v.get("baseline_done")),
                          signals=len(s["signals"]), missed=len(s["missed"]), open=len(s["open"]),
                          closed=len(s["closed"]), errors=len(s.get("errors", [])),
                          last_ts_min=min((v.get("last_ts") or "") for v in syms.values()),
                          last_ts_max=max((v.get("last_ts") or "") for v in syms.values()))
          res = []
          for n in (1, 2):
              log.clear(); t0 = time.time(); code = 0
              try:
                  m.main()

              el = time.time() - t0
              lat = [x[1] for x in log]
              cnt = collections.Counter(str(x[0]) for x in log)
              hdr = next((x[2] for x in log if x[2].get("gw-ratelimit-limit")), {})
              res.append(dict(run=n, exit=code, elapsed_s=round(el, 1), requests=len(log), status=dict(cnt),
                              lat_p50=round(statistics.median(lat), 3), lat_p95=round(sorted(lat)[int(len(lat)*.95)-1], 3), lat_max=round(max(lat), 3),
                              ratelimit_headers=hdr, state=snap()))
              print("PREFLIGHT_RUN", json.dumps(res[-1], sort_keys=True))
              time.sleep(20)
          a, b = res[0]["state"], res[1]["state"]
          cont = dict(created_at_same=a["created_at"] == b["created_at"], eligible_from_same=a["eligible_from"] == b["eligible_from"],
                      baseline_all_after_run1=a["baseline"] == a["n_syms"] == 67, baseline_all_after_run2=b["baseline"] == 67,
                      no_replay_run1=(a["signals"] + a["missed"]) <= 67 and a["open"] == 0 and a["closed"] == 0,
                      run2_new_signals=b["signals"] - a["signals"], run2_new_missed=b["missed"] - a["missed"])
          print("STATE_CONTINUITY", json.dumps(cont, sort_keys=True))
          # bounded burst: 40 back-to-back requests on one symbol, no pause, to observe limit headers / 429
          bl = []
          now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
          for _ in range(40):
              t = time.time()
              r = orig(m.KLINE_URL, params=dict(symbol="XBTUSDTM", granularity=m.GRANULARITY, **{"from": now_ms - 3600*1000*40, "to": now_ms}), timeout=m.REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
              bl.append((r.status_code, round(time.time() - t, 3), r.headers.get("gw-ratelimit-remaining")))
          print("BURST40", json.dumps(dict(status=dict(collections.Counter(str(x[0]) for x in bl)), last_remaining=bl[-1][2], max_lat=max(x[1] for x in bl))))
          ok = all(r["exit"] == 0 for r in res) and all(cont[k] for k in ("created_at_same","eligible_from_same","baseline_all_after_run1","baseline_all_after_run2","no_replay_run1"))
          print("LIVE_RESULT", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
          sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
          PY
          python "$RUNNER_TEMP/pre.py" | tee "$RUNNER_TEMP/live.log"
      - name: Show run summary
        if: always()
        run: |
          { echo '### live'; grep -E "PREFLIGHT_RUN|STATE_CONTINUITY|BURST40|LIVE_RESULT|U67-S|RUN_STATUS|OK=|FAIL" "$RUNNER_TEMP/live.log" | cut -c1-900; } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" || true

  guard:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Test the exact production guard line in a throwaway repo
        run: |
          set -u
          cat > "$RUNNER_TEMP/guard.sh" <<'GUARD'
          if [ -n "$(git log --oneline -1 -- u67_short_paper_state.json)" ] && [ ! -f u67_short_paper_state.json ]; then
            echo "STATE_FILE_MISSING_BUT_IN_HISTORY"
            exit 1
          fi
          GUARD
          G="$(cat "$RUNNER_TEMP/guard.sh")"
          d="$(mktemp -d)"; cd "$d"; git init -q; git config user.name t; git config user.email t@t
          echo x > a; git add a; git commit -qm a
          bash -c "$G"; r1=$?; echo "A no history, no file -> exit $r1 (expect 0)"
          echo '{}' > u67_short_paper_state.json; git add .; git commit -qm s
          bash -c "$G"; r2=$?; echo "B file present -> exit $r2 (expect 0)"
          git rm -q u67_short_paper_state.json; git commit -qm del
          bash -c "$G"; r3=$?; echo "C in history, missing -> exit $r3 (expect 1)"
          [ "$r1" = 0 ] && [ "$r2" = 0 ] && [ "$r3" = 1 ] && echo "GUARD_RESULT PASS" | tee -a "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" || { echo "GUARD_RESULT FAIL" | tee -a "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"; exit 1; }

  race:
    needs: prep
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        who: [c11, longm1, u67, signal]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          ref: u67-preflight-scratch
      - name: Commit and push with the production pattern, simultaneously
        env:
          GO: ${{ needs.prep.outputs.go_epoch }}
          WHO: ${{ matrix.who }}
        run: |
          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
          echo "$WHO $(date +%s%N)" > "race_${WHO}.txt"
          git add "race_${WHO}.txt"
          git commit -qm "race $WHO"
          now=$(date +%s); [ "$GO" -gt "$now" ] && sleep $(( GO - now ))
          echo "START_EPOCH $WHO $(date +%s.%N)"
          if [ "$WHO" = "signal" ]; then
            # existing old-bot pattern: single attempt, no retry
            git pull --rebase --autostash origin "$SCRATCH" && git push origin "HEAD:$SCRATCH" && echo "RACE_RESULT $WHO OK attempts=1(no-retry-pattern)" || { echo "RACE_RESULT $WHO FAIL attempts=1(no-retry-pattern)"; exit 1; }
          else
            ok=0
            for i in 1 2 3; do
              if git pull --rebase origin "$SCRATCH" && git push origin "HEAD:$SCRATCH"; then ok=1; echo "RACE_RESULT $WHO OK attempts=$i"; break; fi
              git rebase --abort 2>/dev/null || true
              sleep 5
            done
            [ "$ok" = "1" ] || { echo "RACE_RESULT $WHO FAIL PUSH_FAILED_AFTER_3_ATTEMPTS"; exit 1; }
          fi

  verify:
    needs: [prep, live, guard, race]
    if: always()
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          ref: u67-preflight-scratch
          fetch-depth: 0
      - name: Verify scratch branch and measure dispatch/queue latency

          echo "RACE_FILES_COUNT $(ls race_*.txt 2>/dev/null | wc -l) (expect 4 if all pushed; 'signal' may legitimately fail: no-retry pattern)"
          echo "MERGE_COMMITS_ON_BRANCH $(git rev-list --merges origin/$SCRATCH --count 2>/dev/null || git rev-list --merges HEAD --count)"
          echo "--- dispatch/queue latency (run created_at vs job started_at)"
          gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}/jobs" --jq '.jobs[] | [.name, .created_at, .started_at] | @tsv' || true
          gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}" --jq '"RUN created_at=\(.created_at) run_started_at=\(.run_started_at) event=\(.event)"' || true
      - name: Delete scratch branch
        if: always()
        run: git push origin --delete "$SCRATCH" || true

name: U67 Preflight 2 (one-shot, isolated)

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      mode:
        description: "controller (default) or probe (used internally)"
        required: false
        default: "controller"

env:

  SCRATCH: u67-pf2-scratch

jobs:
  probe:
    if: ${{ inputs.mode == 'probe' }}

    steps:

        run: |

          sleep 70
          echo "PROBE_END $(date -u +%H:%M:%S)"

    if: ${{ inputs.mode != 'probe' }}

    outputs:
      go_epoch: ${{ steps.t.outputs.go_epoch }}
    steps:

        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

        run: |

          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
          git push origin --delete "$SCRATCH" 2>/dev/null || true
          git push origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$SCRATCH"
      - id: t
        run: echo "go_epoch=$(( $(date +%s) + 110 ))" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

  live_l:
    if: ${{ inputs.mode != 'probe' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install pandas numpy requests
      - name: Verify L engine hash
        run: echo "$L_ENGINE_SHA256  crypto_signal_bot_u67_long_v1.py" | sha256sum -c -
      - name: Live KuCoin, two U67-L engine runs on isolated temp state
        env:
          U67L_STATE_FILE: ${{ runner.temp }}/u67l_pre_state.json
        run: |
          cat > "$RUNNER_TEMP/pre.py" <<'PY'
          import importlib.util, json, os, sys, time, statistics, collections
          spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("u67l", "crypto_signal_bot_u67_long_v1.py")
          m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
          log = []
          orig = m.SESSION.get
          def wrapped(*a, **k):
              t = time.time()
              try:
                  r = orig(*a, **k)
                  log.append((r.status_code, time.time() - t, dict((h, r.headers.get(h)) for h in ("gw-ratelimit-limit","gw-ratelimit-remaining","gw-ratelimit-reset","retry-after"))))
                  return r
              except Exception as e:
                  log.append((type(e).__name__, time.time() - t, {}))
                  raise
          m.SESSION.get = wrapped
          sf = os.environ["U67L_STATE_FILE"]
          def snap():
              s = json.load(open(sf)); syms = s["symbols"]
              return dict(strategy=s["strategy"], created_at=s["created_at"], eligible_from=s["eligible_from"], n_syms=len(syms),
                          baseline=sum(1 for v in syms.values() if v.get("baseline_done")), signals=len(s["signals"]), missed=len(s["missed"]),
                          open=len(s["open"]), closed=len(s["closed"]), errors=len(s.get("errors", [])),
                          last_ts_min=min((v.get("last_ts") or "") for v in syms.values()), last_ts_max=max((v.get("last_ts") or "") for v in syms.values()))
          res = []
          for n in (1, 2):
              log.clear(); t0 = time.time(); code = 0
              try: m.main()
              except SystemExit as e: code = e.code if isinstance(e.code, int) else 1
              lat = [x[1] for x in log]
              res.append(dict(run=n, exit=code, elapsed_s=round(time.time() - t0, 1), requests=len(log), status=dict(collections.Counter(str(x[0]) for x in log)),
                              lat_p50=round(statistics.median(lat), 3), lat_max=round(max(lat), 3), state=snap()))
              print("PREFLIGHT2_RUN", json.dumps(res[-1], sort_keys=True))
              time.sleep(20)
          a, b = res[0]["state"], res[1]["state"]
          cont = dict(strategy_ok=a["strategy"] == "u67_long_only_frozen_v1", created_at_same=a["created_at"] == b["created_at"], eligible_from_same=a["eligible_from"] == b["eligible_from"],
                      eligible_is_registered_literal=a["eligible_from"].startswith("2026-10-30T12:00:00"),
                      baseline_all_after_run1=a["baseline"] == 67, baseline_all_after_run2=b["baseline"] == 67,
                      no_replay_run1=(a["signals"] + a["missed"]) <= 67 and a["open"] == 0 and a["closed"] == 0,
                      run2_new_signals=b["signals"] - a["signals"], run2_new_missed=b["missed"] - a["missed"])
          print("STATE_CONTINUITY_L", json.dumps(cont, sort_keys=True))
          ok = all(r["exit"] == 0 for r in res) and all(cont[k] for k in ("strategy_ok","created_at_same","eligible_from_same","eligible_is_registered_literal","baseline_all_after_run1","baseline_all_after_run2","no_replay_run1"))
          print("LIVE_L_RESULT", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
          sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)
          PY
          python "$RUNNER_TEMP/pre.py" | tee "$RUNNER_TEMP/live.log"
      - name: Summary
        if: always()
        run: |

  guard:
