===== BEGIN FILE u67_audit/registrations/U67_L_REGISTRATION_v1.txt sha256=3f0298f2314d1dc54c7768dbe181b41839e5223f9903356b7a1b903f2af5b02a bytes=38197 =====
U67-L PRE-REGISTRATION v1 (FROZEN)
Track: "U67-L" = the LONG-M1 rules (the exact mirror of Candidate 11) on the independent 67-symbol universe of U67-S. Paper only. No live orders, no real money.
Authorization: designer ruling "U67 Designer Ruling: Option B, SHORT First; LONG After Readiness" (2026-10-28) and the designer instruction of 2026-10-29 (23:49 Tehran) to prepare U67-L independently ("Use the same frozen 67-symbol universe and the exact registered LONG-M1 strategy rules, while keeping LONG-M1 itself unchanged ... Do not deploy U67-L until its own gates pass"). This registration authorizes preparation only; deployment needs the designer's separate readiness decision.
Independence: separate registration, counter, state, workflow, concurrency group and dispatch job. No trade, cluster or outcome is ever pooled with U67-S (same 67 symbols, SHORT), Candidate 11 (C11, 33 symbols), LONG-M1 (33 symbols) or any other track; LONG and SHORT results are never pooled or netted. U67-L reuses the frozen 67-symbol universe of U67-S unchanged. Freeze timestamp (UTC): 2026-10-30T03:40:00Z. ELIGIBLE_FROM (UTC, literal, equal to the engine constant REGISTRATION_ELIGIBLE_FROM, strictly later than the freeze): 2026-10-30T12:00:00Z.
Integrity: the sha256 of this exact text is recorded OUTSIDE this file (registrar's freeze message and the designer's readiness review). A text whose sha256 differs is not this registration.

0. LEAKAGE AND DISCLOSURE STATEMENT
a) NOT done by the registrar: no historical simulation, no signal count, no outcome, no plot or table of any LONG-M1-rule or C11-rule result on the 67 symbols. Only market metadata (24h turnover, contract fields, completed-candle counts and contiguity) and, on 2026-10-29, KuCoin listing announcements for the 14 ambiguous tickers (Appendix B) were read.
b) Inherited and disclosed: every strategy parameter equals LONG-M1's (registration sha256 2ba707dbf6b57c82df38e848230f8fe4606b2a3807619bf8bf13aec792e56ffa), which is the mirror of C11's; they were originally chosen on the 33-symbol universe with SHORT results on historical data (backtest, holdout, forward). U67-L is a mirror-symmetry prior applied to different symbols; it carries NO tuning on the new symbols and no LONG-specific tuning. Status seen by the registrar at the freeze, counts only: LONG-M1 forward 0 signals and 0 closed; C11 forward signals 18, closed 12, open 6; no R value of any track was read or used. U67-S is not deployed, so no U67-S outcome exists.
c) Prior exposure of the new symbols to OTHER studies (different strategies/purposes; the registrar did not run the C11 rule on them): 13 of the 67 appear in the MicroMAP snapshot/data-diagnostic workflows (microstructure data pulls), 48 appear in a V4-SMC metadata audit, 11 in the old V3 bot signals log (signals_log.json); a few one-letter matches (S, MET, H) in other files may be false matches. Counts are by text match of the symbol strings in repository files (heuristic; one-letter symbols may give false matches). Exposure of the 67 symbols to those studies was never an outcome exposure of the C11 or LONG-M1 rule by the registrar. If the designer knows of any C11-rule or LONG-M1-rule result previously seen on any of the 67 symbols, he may state it as an addendum; an addendum does not alter any rule.
d) Selection timing (explicit): the contracts snapshot was taken 2026-10-28T05:26:56.667Z-05:26:57.612Z, i.e. BEFORE this registration's freeze, by a one-shot read-only GitHub Actions run. The exclusion of non-crypto-underlying contracts was decided AFTER inspecting the selection metadata (symbol names and turnover), by the designer's ruling. No outcome data of any kind was retrieved or used at any step. The universe is reused from U67-S unchanged; the verification of the 14 ambiguous tickers (Appendix B) was done on 2026-10-29, after the U67-S freeze, and changed nothing.
e) Evaluation uses only forward data: trades whose entry time is at or after the later of (i) 2026-10-30T12:00:00Z and (ii) the engine state's created_at. Earlier signals are never backfilled or replayed.

1. HYPOTHESIS (one, no alternatives)
On 4H candles of the 67 KuCoin USDT-perpetual crypto symbols in section 2, when price is in an uptrend (close above EMA200), has just made a statistically extreme downward close (z-score of close over 20 bars <= -2) and the first completed candle returns above -2 on above-median range and above-median volume, a LONG entered at the next candle open with stop 1.25 ATR20 and target 2R, held at most 30 candles, has positive expectancy after the S3 cost scenario. "Above-median range" means range20 >= rolling median (20) of range20; "above-median volume" means volume >= rolling median (20) of volume. This is the exact LONG-M1 hypothesis applied to the symbol set of U67-S (the mirror of the U67-S hypothesis on the same symbols). Disjoint from C11/LONG-M1 means no shared symbols; it does NOT mean independent in time or market regime: U67-L runs in the same calendar window as C11, LONG-M1 and U67-S, and altcoins share BTC beta. U67-L and U67-S share the same 67 symbols by design, so a symbol can carry opposite paper positions in the two tracks (never netted, never pooled). Falsifiable by section 8.

2. UNIVERSE (frozen list, never edited)
Universe: identical to section 2 of the U67-S registration (sha256 9efe88d1b07c166be1e4e371e3c2339fc941194b22f8cf03fd5c8d8fe879d8be), reused without any change; the snapshot, ranking, exclusions, list and hashes below are copied from it.
Snapshot: contracts/active, KuCoin Futures, 2026-10-28T05:26:56.667Z-05:26:57.612Z; 712 contracts; raw response sha256 6176f7deb0c78c08eeca342a3dfdd185e84d6cee4e943e0c1dba559dbf9d0f02. Recorded in repo file u67_universe_snapshot.json (sha256 3e9f1ba5903c3c73fe44f5c74e3f8aeddb2e046d7daa9cb43851de9e3930cc2a, commit feb4261).
Static eligibility (unchanged from the first selection): quote=settle=USDT, not inverse, status Open, no expireDate/settleDate, symbol ends USDTM, base currency not in {USDT USDC DAI TUSD FDUSD USDD USDE PYUSD BUSD USDP USD1 EURC EURT USDS USDG RLUSD}, 24h turnover > 0; the 33 symbols of C11/LONG-M1 excluded. Ranking: turnoverOf24h descending, tie by symbol ascending (667 contracts pass the static rules).
Candle rule (engine's own): at least 250 completed 4H candles AND the last 250 contiguous (check_contiguous). Clarification vs "at least 250 completed": contiguity is required because the engine errors on a gapped symbol in every run.
Exclusion policy (designer, Q1 option B): non-crypto or unverified underlying excluded: PAXGUSDTM XAUTUSDTM XAGUSDTM MUUSDTM SNDKUSDTM SKHYNIXUSDTM CRCLUSDTM SOXLUSDTM SOXSUSDTM SPCXUSDTM. The same policy applies to any replacement candidate whose underlying is DEMONSTRABLY non-crypto; default for everything else is crypto (burden of proof lies on exclusion). Evidence = KuCoin listing announcements (stock-index/commodity categories). Partial tables are in u67_universe_final.json (non_crypto_evidenced, crypto_channel_evidence); the complete per-symbol table for the final 67 is Appendix A of this document and is authoritative. Classification is by the listed project/underlying, not by ticker spelling (several crypto tickers coincide with equity tickers, e.g. M, S, H, MET). Candidates were considered only up to turnover rank 100, an operational limit of the script that was never reached (67 eligible symbols were obtained at rank 80); beyond it no classification exists and the process would have stopped with a shortfall report. Per-symbol classification evidence is in Appendix A of this document.
Re-selection (designer Q2): from the SAME snapshot and ranking, no new snapshot; candle-check in rank order; the first 57 survivors of ranks 1-69 were kept (results of the original run 2026-10-28T05:28Z), 10 replacements were candle-checked 2026-10-28T05:48Z in ranks 70-80.
Rejections/exclusions recorded: PAXGUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), TSLAUSDTM (NON_CRYPTO_EVIDENCED), MUUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SNDKUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SOXLUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SPCXUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SKHYNIXUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), CRCLUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SOXSUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), XAGUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), XAUTUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED); rejected on the candle rule: CTUSDTM (CANDLE_RULE:INSUFFICIENT_CANDLES:167), AXISUSDTM (CANDLE_RULE:INSUFFICIENT_CANDLES:36).
Final ordered list (67, selection order = rank order), sha256 of the newline-joined list = 5123e95389eaab880b202b740ce9c7bc54f5a40451e4d40b741ac210e4d21192; recorded in u67_universe_final.json (sha256 96ca3e68fb1d79847e1e19fa9c3889d3212705860ba94fd81ed2050ed2a8d21b, commit 31ff38b1a5b30aec6949ff8b45493d55eba5c910). The first-pass list before the designer's exclusion had sha256 ea264a7efa0685b6b5cedfc74a9f7eb8cf219e6531718d73334e283022e19a33 (u67_universe_snapshot.json commit feb426125c3d82c5868eb2d7e49a9c976cd3014d). Selection code commits: 108bc261650c37af3be51de4527302b670207ead (reselect script), a3858e3f462c18df0115e2f2ea7a9a365b5d10f1 (selection workflow), 8ad0dfc918d58e4c6f975931e1e6e863ccb8cab7 (reselect workflow).

  EDENUSDTM
Universe integrity: no symbol is ever added, removed or replaced; eligibility is re-tested by the engine every run and a symbol that later fails is not edited out. A symbol that is delisted, halted or persistently erroring is reported (counts only) and produces no signals; the verdict stands on the symbols that remain available. Engine behavior stated precisely: a symbol whose last 250 completed candles contain a gap fails that run; its cursor (last_ts) does not advance, so monitoring catches up when data is clean again, and any catch-up signal on a candle processed more than 90 minutes after its entry time is recorded MISSED. Signals on candles before the last completed candle of a symbol's first successful run are not recorded at all (no replay, as in C11).
UNRESOLVED positions (evaluation-time rule, mechanical, price-independent; NO engine change): at the evaluation instant a position that is still in the state's open set is declared UNRESOLVED if the instant minus the cursor symbols[<symbol>].last_ts of its symbol (last successfully processed candle) is at least 45 days. 45 days exceeds the engine's own 250-bar (about 41.7 days) contiguity-recovery window and the 5-day maximum holding. Status is fixed at the evaluation instant: a position closed by the engine before that instant keeps its engine outcome; after the instant the fixed checkpoint is never recomputed. An UNRESOLVED position is counted conservatively as a stop-out in EVERY scenario (S0, S1, S2, S3, S3a, S3b): gross R = -1, exit price = stop, bars_held = 30, each scenario's own cost formula with the stored entry, risk and stop. It is flagged in the report and a sensitivity excluding it is shown. Fields used: open[].symbol/entry/risk/sl/signal_ts and symbols[].last_ts, all already stored by the engine. The rule is computed by the evaluator, never written into the engine state.
Run health (engine rule): a run is DEGRADED when MORE than 20% of the 67 symbols fail in that run, i.e. 14 or more (13/67 = 19.4% is not degraded; 14/67 = 20.9% is). A degraded run saves state, skips the report and exits non-zero (the workflow then fails and alerts). Degradation is operational only: it voids no trade, no cluster and no checkpoint.
State continuity: the git history of u67_long_paper_state.json is the record. If the state file is ever lost or recreated, it must be restored from the last committed version; a silently recreated state (the engine creates a new one with a later eligible_from) is a defect, not a restart, and the affected period is labeled. Eligibility compares the trade's entry_ts, not the signal time, with eligible_from.
Log retention: the engine keeps bounded lists (errors 100, missed 500, signals 500, closed 5000). Full history is preserved in git (every state change is committed). Closed trades are never truncated before UL2 (expected volume is far below 5000).
Attrition of the universe is reported in the registrar's daily counts-only review (a review practice, not an engine feature).

3. INDICATORS (identical to C11 prepare())
ATR20 = EWM(alpha=1/20, adjust=False) of true range. z20 = (close - SMA20(close)) / STD20(close, ddof=1); std 0 -> NaN. EMA200 = close EWM(span=200, adjust=False). range20 = rolling max high 20 - rolling min low 20; range_median20 = rolling median 20 of range20. vol_median20 = rolling median 20 of volume. Any NaN -> no signal.

4. SIGNAL (candle t completed, all must hold)
(1) z20[t-1] <= -2.0 and z20[t] > -2.0
(2) close[t] > EMA200[t]
(3) range20[t] >= range_median20[t]
(4) volume[t] >= vol_median20[t]
(5) ATR20[t] finite and > 0

5. TRADE RULES (identical to LONG-M1)
Entry: open of candle t+1 (entry_ts = signal_ts + 4h). If the engine sees it more than 90 minutes after entry_ts: MISSED (ENTRY_DELAY_EXCEEDED), never backfilled, never reclassified. Entry candle missing/invalid: MISSED. MIN_CANDLES = 250.
Risk = 1.25 x ATR20[t]; stop = entry - risk; target = entry + 2 x risk; risk_pct = risk/entry. No risk_pct filter.
One open position per symbol; a second signal while open is recorded MISSED (SYMBOL_ALREADY_OPEN). No portfolio cap; maximum concurrent open trades is reported.
Monitoring (entry candle counts as bar 1): per candle, stop first (low <= stop -> gross R = -1), then target (high >= target -> gross R = +2), else at bar 30 exit at that candle's close, gross R = (close - entry)/risk. Stop fills at the stop level and target at the target level (paper model; optimistic in gaps; see stop_gap). exit_ts = open time of the exit candle; bars_held counts entry and exit candles. stop_gap = (exit_reason == SL and the exit candle's open <= stop); recorded only, never alters the outcome.
No parameter, filter, symbol or rule changes during the test (section 9).

6. COSTS AND NET R
net R = gross R - cost_R; all costs are fractions of entry notional; cost_R = total fraction / risk_pct.
S0 (state field net_r): flat 0.20% round trip, no funding.
S3 (DECISION scenario, state field net_r_s3): fee 0.15% per side: 0.0015 + 0.0015 x (exit_price/entry); funding assumed 0.010% per 8h charged as a cost on entry notional: 0.00010 x bars_held x 0.5 (upper bound: holding = bars_held x 4h); no slippage. The funding is a flat assumption, labeled "assumed", and is NOT a bound: for hype or newly listed symbols real funding can be several times larger, and longs can pay several times more during crowded rallies. For longs positive funding is normally paid, so the assumed charge is realistic in ordinary conditions and understated when funding is high; it is charged as a cost in every case (conservative only when funding is negative). Real funding is not measured (S4 needs the designer's authorization).
S3a / S3b (sensitivities only): S3 with 0.00025 x (1 + exit_price/entry) / 0.0005 x (1 + exit_price/entry) added to the total cost fraction (0.05% / 0.10% round trip, split equally between entry and exit). Smaller symbols may have higher real slippage; S3 contains none by the designer's ruling. S3a/S3b are reported on every checkpoint; if S3 passes while S3a or S3b does not, that is reported descriptively and changes no verdict.
S1: all-market fee 0.0006 x (1 + exit_price/entry), no funding. S2: S1, except trades with exit_reason == TP use 0.0004 x (1 + exit_price/entry). No funding. S1/S2 transparency only.
S4 (measured funding) only if the designer authorizes funding-history access. Paper results are not proof of real execution or profitability.

7. CLUSTERS, CHECKPOINTS, STATISTICS (as LONG-M1 section 7, adapted)
Cluster = all eligible trades sharing the same signal-candle timestamp (strict count; primary). Sensitivity: adjacent consecutive 4H signal candles merged. Cluster outcome = equal-weight mean net R of its closed trades; the cluster-level mean (equal weight per cluster) is the primary statistic. Eligible trades only (section 0e); MISSED entries are reported separately, not counted, not backfilled.
Cluster formation: a cluster contains only OPENED eligible trades with the same signal-candle timestamp; MISSED signals never create, join or alter a cluster. All trades of one signal candle are opened within 90 minutes after its entry time (later processing is MISSED), so a cluster is complete 90 minutes after its entry_ts. Clusters are ordered by signal_ts.
Selection of clusters (no cherry-picking): UL1 uses the FIRST 15 strict clusters ordered by signal_ts; UL2 uses the FIRST 30 (cumulative, includes the UL1 clusters). A checkpoint is evaluated exactly once, on the FIRST committed state in the repository's git history in which the first N clusters (N=15 for UL1, 30 for UL2) all exist, are complete and have zero open trades (every trade closed by TP, SL or 30-bar timeout, or counted as UNRESOLVED relative to that commit's time). The evaluation instant is the commit time of that state, found by the evaluator scanning the history from the first state commit onward; the registrar's review time is irrelevant and he has no discretion over it. The trigger is detected from counts only; no R value is viewed before it holds. The cluster-timestamp set is then fixed; degraded runs do not matter because evaluation reads committed states, not runs. The evaluator (cluster building, bootstrap, jackknife, UNRESOLVED rule, and a check that the state's created_at equals that of the first committed state; a mismatch labels the period as a defect) is a single script committed to the repo, tested on synthetic data only, and its sha256 is recorded in the repository before 5 clusters exist; any later change to it follows section 9. Later clusters are excluded from that checkpoint and its statistics even if they close earlier. Open trades outside those clusters are reported, not counted.
No outcome review before UL1; operational status (counts of signals, open, closed, missed, errors, symbol health) may be reported earlier, R values are not reported or acted upon earlier.
Statistics (fixed): cluster-mean net R by S0, S3, S3a, S3b (S1, S2 labeled, transparency only); 95% CI = percentile 2.5/97.5 of the mean of cluster means, resampling strict clusters with replacement, 20000 draws, numpy default_rng seed 20261029; P(mean>0) descriptive only. Also: trade-level mean, profit factor ("n/a" if no losses), max drawdown in R (trades ordered by exit_ts, ties by signal_ts then symbol), maximum concurrent open trades; breakdown by calendar month (month of signal_ts, UTC) and by symbol; BTC change over the window as one descriptive label (no filter).
Jackknife definitions: largest cluster = the cluster with the highest S3 cluster-mean net R, i.e. the largest contributor to the primary statistic (ties to the earlier signal_ts). Note: LONG-M1 section 7 wording ("highest S3 net R sum") is ambiguous between a trade-level sum and the cluster-mean; for U67-L the cluster-mean reading is registered; best month = the calendar month (signal_ts UTC) with the highest S3 net R sum of cluster outcomes (ties to the earlier). Each is removed once; the remaining statistic is the equal-weight mean of the remaining cluster means. If a removal leaves zero clusters, that jackknife mean is treated as <= 0.
Rate cap: if the UL1 condition (section 7, first 15 clusters complete and fully closed) is not met 120 days after the section 0e eligibility boundary, or the UL2 condition is not met 240 days after it, the observed rate is reported; no verdict. The only permitted actions are (a) continue unchanged or (b) stop the track; the choice is made once per cap, recorded before any R value is viewed, and rules, symbols and parameters are never altered.

8. DECISION RULES (decided now; S3 is the sole decision scenario)
- UL1 (15 clusters): only the rejection rule is binding. If S3 cluster-mean <= 0: REJECTED, the track is stopped; the designer may only archive it or register a new hypothesis (new registration, section 9); rules are not altered and not retested on the same observations. If S3 cluster-mean > 0: continue to UL2 with no verdict; CI and jackknife are descriptive at UL1.
- UL2 (30 clusters): REJECTED as above if S3 cluster-mean <= 0. NOT ESTABLISHED if S3 mean > 0 but CI95 lower bound <= 0, or removing the largest cluster or the best month leaves a mean <= 0. NOT ESTABLISHED is final for U67-L v1: no extension, no additional clusters, no re-evaluation; any further data requires a new registration. PAPER GATE PASSED only if S3 cluster-mean > 0 AND CI95 lower bound > 0 AND mean without the largest cluster > 0 AND mean without the best month > 0. The paper gate authorizes nothing but a further review; a real-money pilot always needs a separate explicit authorization per track and per direction.
- Tracks are judged on their own. U67-L results are never combined with U67-S, C11, LONG-M1 or any other track; opposite positions on the same symbol in different tracks (including U67-S, which shares the same 67 symbols) are not netted. Symbols are disjoint between U67-L and C11/LONG-M1 by construction; U67-L and U67-S share their symbols by design.
Decision-rule note (designer ruling 3a, 2026-10-28/29): UL1 keeps the binding REJECTED wording of the LONG-M1 registration and of U67-S; the C11 review-flag semantics are not used. The statistic is unchanged; only the consequence at UL1 is binding.
Limits of inference (stated now): clusters are treated as exchangeable in the bootstrap, but adjacent 4H clusters are dependent (the adjacent-merge sensitivity shows this); the confidence interval is conditional on that assumption. The 15-cluster rejection test and the 30-cluster test reuse the first 15 clusters, so no multiplicity correction is made. The universe is the top of one day's turnover ranking, which favors currently active names. U67-L and U67-S use the same 67 symbols in the same window, so their clusters are exposed to the same market moves; they are judged separately and never pooled.

9. CHANGE CONTROL
Any change to rules, parameters, symbols or classification = a new registration with a new counter; old evidence is kept, labeled, never pooled. Code defects are fixed only to restore registered behavior; each fix is documented with its commit and the affected period labeled. No tuning of any kind on observed results.

10. IMPLEMENTATION CONSTRAINTS
Separate files only: crypto_signal_bot_u67_long_v1.py, u67_long_paper_state.json, .github/workflows/paper_u67_long_v1.yml (concurrency group u67-long-paper), own external dispatch job (proposed minutes 26 and 56 of hours 0,4,8,12,16,20 UTC, staggered from C11 :05/:35, LONG-M1 :12/:42 and U67-S :19/:49; the schedule is operational, not part of the hypothesis). Engine = the LONG-M1 engine (crypto_signal_bot_long_v1.py, sha256 2a3f602c7ac139ce8683ed7dc9875ff04ce7c0d1d4ee670eba591a66151ea0e3) with only these differences: SYMS (the frozen 67), STATE_FILE and state/strategy names, the REGISTRATION_ELIGIBLE_FROM literal, user-agent string and header comments. Workflow = the U67-S workflow with its own names/group/paths (documented): checkout with full history (fetch-depth 0) and one guard step before the engine run: if the state file has ever been committed (git history) but is absent in the checkout, the workflow fails instead of letting the engine silently create a new state. Evaluation (cluster building, bootstrap, jackknife, UNRESOLVED rule) is done by a separate evaluator script evaluate_u67_long.py, committed with its tests, test log and sha256 manifest before 5 clusters exist, never by the engine. Tests use synthetic data only; no historical market data is run through the strategy. Not deployed before the designer's readiness review. LONG-M1, U67-S, C11 and the selection files are not modified.

APPENDIX A - CLASSIFICATION EVIDENCE (registered with this text)
Method: (1) every contract symbol named in the KuCoin TradFi announcements retrieved on 2026-10-28 was collected (80 base names; non-exhaustive: KuCoin states 170+ TradFi contracts); (2) the 67 selected symbols were checked against that set: ZERO matches (computed); (3) for the symbols below a crypto-style KuCoin new-listing announcement was located by title search. Symbols without an entry in (3) rest on (2) plus the default rule (no evidence of a non-crypto underlying); the registrar states plainly that for those the evidence is absence of non-crypto evidence, not a positive identification, and that the TradFi announcement set is not guaranteed complete.
Ambiguous-ticker note: the symbols 4, M, S, H, Q, MET, ARC, CAP, APR, WET, BEAT, CLO, ZBT, JCT share their ticker with, or look like, equity or index tickers. Each was verified on 2026-10-29 against KuCoin's own listing announcements/pages as a crypto asset (Appendix B); the per-symbol table below is the evidence recorded at the U67-S freeze and is unchanged. The designer may veto any symbol before deployment; a veto means a new version with a new hash.

===== BEGIN FILE u67_audit/registrations/U67_S_REGISTRATION_v1.txt sha256=9efe88d1b07c166be1e4e371e3c2339fc941194b22f8cf03fd5c8d8fe879d8be bytes=33151 =====
U67-S PRE-REGISTRATION v1 (FROZEN)
Track: "U67-S" = Candidate-11 SHORT rules on an independent 67-symbol universe. Paper only. No live orders, no real money.
Authorization: designer message "Authorization for Independent 67-Symbol SHORT Paper Track" and ruling "U67 Designer Ruling: Option B, SHORT First; LONG After Readiness" (2026-10-28).
Independence: separate registration, counter, state, workflow, concurrency group and dispatch job. No trade, cluster or outcome is ever pooled with Candidate 11 (C11, 33 symbols), LONG-M1 (33 symbols) or any other track. Freeze timestamp (UTC): 2026-10-28T10:06:00Z. ELIGIBLE_FROM (UTC, literal, equal to the engine constant REGISTRATION_ELIGIBLE_FROM, strictly later than the freeze): 2026-10-28T12:00:00Z.
Integrity: the sha256 of this exact text is recorded OUTSIDE this file (registrar's freeze message and the designer's readiness review). A text whose sha256 differs is not this registration.

0. LEAKAGE AND DISCLOSURE STATEMENT
a) NOT done by the registrar: no historical simulation, no signal count, no outcome, no plot or table of any C11-rule result on the 67 symbols. Only market metadata (24h turnover, contract fields, completed-candle counts and contiguity) was read to define the universe.
b) Inherited and disclosed: every strategy parameter equals C11's and was originally chosen on the 33-symbol universe with SHORT results on historical data (backtest, holdout, forward). U67-S therefore tests generalization of a frozen rule to different symbols; it carries NO tuning on the new symbols.
c) Prior exposure of the new symbols to OTHER studies (different strategies/purposes; the registrar did not run the C11 rule on them): 13 of the 67 appear in the MicroMAP snapshot/data-diagnostic workflows (microstructure data pulls), 48 appear in a V4-SMC metadata audit, 11 in the old V3 bot signals log (signals_log.json); a few one-letter matches (S, MET, H) in other files may be false matches. Counts are by text match of the symbol strings in repository files (heuristic; one-letter symbols may give false matches). Exposure of the 67 symbols to those studies was never an outcome exposure of the C11 SHORT rule by the registrar. If the designer knows of any C11-rule SHORT result previously seen on any of the 67 symbols, he may state it as an addendum; an addendum does not alter any rule.
d) Selection timing (explicit): the contracts snapshot was taken 2026-10-28T05:26:56.667Z-05:26:57.612Z, i.e. BEFORE this registration's freeze, by a one-shot read-only GitHub Actions run. The exclusion of non-crypto-underlying contracts was decided AFTER inspecting the selection metadata (symbol names and turnover), by the designer's ruling. No outcome data of any kind was retrieved or used at any step.
e) Evaluation uses only forward data: trades whose entry time is at or after the later of (i) 2026-10-28T12:00:00Z and (ii) the engine state's created_at. Earlier signals are never backfilled or replayed.

1. HYPOTHESIS (one, no alternatives)
On 4H candles of the 67 KuCoin USDT-perpetual crypto symbols in section 2, when price is in a downtrend (close below EMA200), has just made a statistically extreme upward close (z-score of close over 20 bars >= +2) and the first completed candle returns below +2 on above-median range and above-median volume, a SHORT entered at the next candle open with stop 1.25 ATR20 and target 2R, held at most 30 candles, has positive expectancy after the S3 cost scenario. This is the exact C11 hypothesis applied to a disjoint symbol set. Disjoint means no shared symbols; it does NOT mean independent in time or market regime: U67-S runs in the same calendar window as C11 and LONG-M1 and altcoins share BTC beta. Falsifiable by section 8.

2. UNIVERSE (frozen list, never edited)
Snapshot: contracts/active, KuCoin Futures, 2026-10-28T05:26:56.667Z-05:26:57.612Z; 712 contracts; raw response sha256 6176f7deb0c78c08eeca342a3dfdd185e84d6cee4e943e0c1dba559dbf9d0f02. Recorded in repo file u67_universe_snapshot.json (sha256 3e9f1ba5903c3c73fe44f5c74e3f8aeddb2e046d7daa9cb43851de9e3930cc2a, commit feb4261).
Static eligibility (unchanged from the first selection): quote=settle=USDT, not inverse, status Open, no expireDate/settleDate, symbol ends USDTM, base currency not in {USDT USDC DAI TUSD FDUSD USDD USDE PYUSD BUSD USDP USD1 EURC EURT USDS USDG RLUSD}, 24h turnover > 0; the 33 symbols of C11/LONG-M1 excluded. Ranking: turnoverOf24h descending, tie by symbol ascending (667 contracts pass the static rules).
Candle rule (engine's own): at least 250 completed 4H candles AND the last 250 contiguous (check_contiguous). Clarification vs "at least 250 completed": contiguity is required because the engine errors on a gapped symbol in every run.
Exclusion policy (designer, Q1 option B): non-crypto or unverified underlying excluded: PAXGUSDTM XAUTUSDTM XAGUSDTM MUUSDTM SNDKUSDTM SKHYNIXUSDTM CRCLUSDTM SOXLUSDTM SOXSUSDTM SPCXUSDTM. The same policy applies to any replacement candidate whose underlying is DEMONSTRABLY non-crypto; default for everything else is crypto (burden of proof lies on exclusion). Evidence = KuCoin listing announcements (stock-index/commodity categories). Partial tables are in u67_universe_final.json (non_crypto_evidenced, crypto_channel_evidence); the complete per-symbol table for the final 67 is Appendix A of this document and is authoritative. Classification is by the listed project/underlying, not by ticker spelling (several crypto tickers coincide with equity tickers, e.g. M, S, H, MET). Candidates were considered only up to turnover rank 100, an operational limit of the script that was never reached (67 eligible symbols were obtained at rank 80); beyond it no classification exists and the process would have stopped with a shortfall report. Per-symbol classification evidence is in Appendix A of this document.
Re-selection (designer Q2): from the SAME snapshot and ranking, no new snapshot; candle-check in rank order; the first 57 survivors of ranks 1-69 were kept (results of the original run 2026-10-28T05:28Z), 10 replacements were candle-checked 2026-10-28T05:48Z in ranks 70-80.
Rejections/exclusions recorded: PAXGUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), TSLAUSDTM (NON_CRYPTO_EVIDENCED), MUUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SNDKUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SOXLUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SPCXUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SKHYNIXUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), CRCLUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), SOXSUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), XAGUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED), XAUTUSDTM (DESIGNER_EXCLUDED_NON_CRYPTO_OR_UNVERIFIED); rejected on the candle rule: CTUSDTM (CANDLE_RULE:INSUFFICIENT_CANDLES:167), AXISUSDTM (CANDLE_RULE:INSUFFICIENT_CANDLES:36).
Final ordered list (67, selection order = rank order), sha256 of the newline-joined list = 5123e95389eaab880b202b740ce9c7bc54f5a40451e4d40b741ac210e4d21192; recorded in u67_universe_final.json (sha256 96ca3e68fb1d79847e1e19fa9c3889d3212705860ba94fd81ed2050ed2a8d21b, commit 31ff38b1a5b30aec6949ff8b45493d55eba5c910). The first-pass list before the designer's exclusion had sha256 ea264a7efa0685b6b5cedfc74a9f7eb8cf219e6531718d73334e283022e19a33 (u67_universe_snapshot.json commit feb426125c3d82c5868eb2d7e49a9c976cd3014d). Selection code commits: 108bc261650c37af3be51de4527302b670207ead (reselect script), a3858e3f462c18df0115e2f2ea7a9a365b5d10f1 (selection workflow), 8ad0dfc918d58e4c6f975931e1e6e863ccb8cab7 (reselect workflow).
  ZECUSDTM IRYSUSDTM EVAAUSDTM HYPEUSDTM KCSUSDTM ASTERUSDTM
  TIAUSDTM USELESSUSDTM QUSDTM XMRUSDTM PENGUUSDTM SOONUSDTM
  LIGHTUSDTM AINUSDTM ENAUSDTM PUMPUSDTM TAOUSDTM LDOUSDTM
  NIGHTUSDTM GRAMUSDTM 1000BONKUSDTM ICNTUSDTM CAKEUSDTM XANUSDTM

  ZORAUSDTM QNTUSDTM MUSDTM CRVUSDTM 0GUSDTM ZBTUSDTM

  ZKPUSDTM ALLOUSDTM AKEUSDTM DASHUSDTM HUSDTM WETUSDTM
  4USDTM PIEVERSEUSDTM KGENUSDTM VIRTUALUSDTM POWERUSDTM CASHCATUSDTM
  AEONUSDTM CHIPUSDTM LONGXIAUSDTM GENIUSUSDTM TRUSTUSDTM FOLKSUSDTM
  EDENUSDTM
Universe integrity: no symbol is ever added, removed or replaced; eligibility is re-tested by the engine every run and a symbol that later fails is not edited out. A symbol that is delisted, halted or persistently erroring is reported (counts only) and produces no signals; the verdict stands on the symbols that remain available. Engine behavior stated precisely: a symbol whose last 250 completed candles contain a gap fails that run; its cursor (last_ts) does not advance, so monitoring catches up when data is clean again, and any catch-up signal on a candle processed more than 90 minutes after its entry time is recorded MISSED. Signals on candles before the last completed candle of a symbol's first successful run are not recorded at all (no replay, as in C11).
UNRESOLVED positions (evaluation-time rule, mechanical, price-independent; NO engine change): at the evaluation instant a position that is still in the state's open set is declared UNRESOLVED if the instant minus the cursor symbols[<symbol>].last_ts of its symbol (last successfully processed candle) is at least 45 days. 45 days exceeds the engine's own 250-bar (about 41.7 days) contiguity-recovery window and the 5-day maximum holding. Status is fixed at the evaluation instant: a position closed by the engine before that instant keeps its engine outcome; after the instant the fixed checkpoint is never recomputed. An UNRESOLVED position is counted conservatively as a stop-out in EVERY scenario (S0, S1, S2, S3, S3a, S3b): gross R = -1, exit price = stop, bars_held = 30, each scenario's own cost formula with the stored entry, risk and stop. It is flagged in the report and a sensitivity excluding it is shown. Fields used: open[].symbol/entry/risk/sl/signal_ts and symbols[].last_ts, all already stored by the engine. The rule is computed by the evaluator, never written into the engine state.
Run health (engine rule): a run is DEGRADED when MORE than 20% of the 67 symbols fail in that run, i.e. 14 or more (13/67 = 19.4% is not degraded; 14/67 = 20.9% is). A degraded run saves state, skips the report and exits non-zero (the workflow then fails and alerts). Degradation is operational only: it voids no trade, no cluster and no checkpoint.
State continuity: the git history of u67_short_paper_state.json is the record. If the state file is ever lost or recreated, it must be restored from the last committed version; a silently recreated state (the engine creates a new one with a later eligible_from) is a defect, not a restart, and the affected period is labeled. Eligibility compares the trade's entry_ts, not the signal time, with eligible_from.
Log retention: the engine keeps bounded lists (errors 100, missed 500, signals 500, closed 5000). Full history is preserved in git (every state change is committed). Closed trades are never truncated before UC2 (expected volume is far below 5000).
Attrition of the universe is reported in the registrar's daily counts-only review (a review practice, not an engine feature).

3. INDICATORS (identical to C11 prepare())
ATR20 = EWM(alpha=1/20, adjust=False) of true range. z20 = (close - SMA20(close)) / STD20(close, ddof=1); std 0 -> NaN. EMA200 = close EWM(span=200, adjust=False). range20 = rolling max high 20 - rolling min low 20; range_median20 = rolling median 20 of range20. vol_median20 = rolling median 20 of volume. Any NaN -> no signal.

4. SIGNAL (candle t completed, all must hold)
(1) z20[t-1] >= +2.0 and z20[t] < +2.0
(2) close[t] < EMA200[t]
(3) range20[t] >= range_median20[t]
(4) volume[t] >= vol_median20[t]
(5) ATR20[t] finite and > 0

5. TRADE RULES (identical to C11)
Entry: open of candle t+1 (entry_ts = signal_ts + 4h). If the engine sees it more than 90 minutes after entry_ts: MISSED (ENTRY_DELAY_EXCEEDED), never backfilled, never reclassified. Entry candle missing/invalid: MISSED. MIN_CANDLES = 250.
Risk = 1.25 x ATR20[t]; stop = entry + risk; target = entry - 2 x risk; risk_pct = risk/entry. No risk_pct filter.
One open position per symbol; a second signal while open is recorded MISSED (SYMBOL_ALREADY_OPEN). No portfolio cap; maximum concurrent open trades is reported.
Monitoring (entry candle counts as bar 1): per candle, stop first (high >= stop -> gross R = -1), then target (low <= target -> gross R = +2), else at bar 30 exit at that candle's close, gross R = (entry - close)/risk. Stop fills at the stop level and target at the target level (paper model; optimistic in gaps; see stop_gap). exit_ts = open time of the exit candle; bars_held counts entry and exit candles. stop_gap = (exit_reason == SL and the exit candle's open >= stop); recorded only, never alters the outcome.
No parameter, filter, symbol or rule changes during the test (section 9).

6. COSTS AND NET R
net R = gross R - cost_R; all costs are fractions of entry notional; cost_R = total fraction / risk_pct.
S0 (state field net_r): flat 0.20% round trip, no funding.
S3 (DECISION scenario, state field net_r_s3): fee 0.15% per side: 0.0015 + 0.0015 x (exit_price/entry); funding assumed 0.010% per 8h charged as a cost on entry notional: 0.00010 x bars_held x 0.5 (upper bound: holding = bars_held x 4h); no slippage. The funding is a flat assumption, labeled "assumed", and is NOT a bound: for hype or newly listed symbols real funding can be several times larger, and shorts pay when funding turns negative after a squeeze. For shorts positive funding is normally received, so charging it is conservative in ordinary conditions; it is charged as a cost in every case. Real funding is not measured (S4 needs the designer's authorization).
S3a / S3b (sensitivities only): S3 with 0.00025 x (1 + exit_price/entry) / 0.0005 x (1 + exit_price/entry) added to the total cost fraction (0.05% / 0.10% round trip, split equally between entry and exit). Smaller symbols may have higher real slippage; S3 contains none by the designer's ruling. S3a/S3b are reported on every checkpoint; if S3 passes while S3a or S3b does not, that is reported descriptively and changes no verdict.
S1: all-market fee 0.0006 x (1 + exit_price/entry), no funding. S2: S1, except trades with exit_reason == TP use 0.0004 x (1 + exit_price/entry). No funding. S1/S2 transparency only.
S4 (measured funding) only if the designer authorizes funding-history access. Paper results are not proof of real execution or profitability.

7. CLUSTERS, CHECKPOINTS, STATISTICS (as LONG-M1 section 7, adapted)
Cluster = all eligible trades sharing the same signal-candle timestamp (strict count; primary). Sensitivity: adjacent consecutive 4H signal candles merged. Cluster outcome = equal-weight mean net R of its closed trades; the cluster-level mean (equal weight per cluster) is the primary statistic. Eligible trades only (section 0e); MISSED entries are reported separately, not counted, not backfilled.
Cluster formation: a cluster contains only OPENED eligible trades with the same signal-candle timestamp; MISSED signals never create, join or alter a cluster. All trades of one signal candle are opened within 90 minutes after its entry time (later processing is MISSED), so a cluster is complete 90 minutes after its entry_ts. Clusters are ordered by signal_ts.
Selection of clusters (no cherry-picking): UC1 uses the FIRST 15 strict clusters ordered by signal_ts; UC2 uses the FIRST 30 (cumulative, includes the UC1 clusters). A checkpoint is evaluated exactly once, on the FIRST committed state in the repository's git history in which the first N clusters (N=15 for UC1, 30 for UC2) all exist, are complete and have zero open trades (every trade closed by TP, SL or 30-bar timeout, or counted as UNRESOLVED relative to that commit's time). The evaluation instant is the commit time of that state, found by the evaluator scanning the history from the first state commit onward; the registrar's review time is irrelevant and he has no discretion over it. The trigger is detected from counts only; no R value is viewed before it holds. The cluster-timestamp set is then fixed; degraded runs do not matter because evaluation reads committed states, not runs. The evaluator (cluster building, bootstrap, jackknife, UNRESOLVED rule, and a check that the state's created_at equals that of the first committed state; a mismatch labels the period as a defect) is a single script committed to the repo, tested on synthetic data only, and its sha256 is recorded in the repository before 5 clusters exist; any later change to it follows section 9. Later clusters are excluded from that checkpoint and its statistics even if they close earlier. Open trades outside those clusters are reported, not counted.
No outcome review before UC1; operational status (counts of signals, open, closed, missed, errors, symbol health) may be reported earlier, R values are not reported or acted upon earlier.
Statistics (fixed): cluster-mean net R by S0, S3, S3a, S3b (S1, S2 labeled, transparency only); 95% CI = percentile 2.5/97.5 of the mean of cluster means, resampling strict clusters with replacement, 20000 draws, numpy default_rng seed 20261028; P(mean>0) descriptive only. Also: trade-level mean, profit factor ("n/a" if no losses), max drawdown in R (trades ordered by exit_ts, ties by signal_ts then symbol), maximum concurrent open trades; breakdown by calendar month (month of signal_ts, UTC) and by symbol; BTC change over the window as one descriptive label (no filter).
Jackknife definitions: largest cluster = the cluster with the highest S3 cluster-mean net R, i.e. the largest contributor to the primary statistic (ties to the earlier signal_ts). Note: LONG-M1 section 7 wording ("highest S3 net R sum") is ambiguous between a trade-level sum and the cluster-mean; for U67-S the cluster-mean reading is registered; best month = the calendar month (signal_ts UTC) with the highest S3 net R sum of cluster outcomes (ties to the earlier). Each is removed once; the remaining statistic is the equal-weight mean of the remaining cluster means. If a removal leaves zero clusters, that jackknife mean is treated as <= 0.
Rate cap: if the UC1 condition (section 7, first 15 clusters complete and fully closed) is not met 120 days after the section 0e eligibility boundary, or the UC2 condition is not met 240 days after it, the observed rate is reported; no verdict. The only permitted actions are (a) continue unchanged or (b) stop the track; the choice is made once per cap, recorded before any R value is viewed, and rules, symbols and parameters are never altered.

8. DECISION RULES (decided now; S3 is the sole decision scenario)
- UC1 (15 clusters): only the rejection rule is binding. If S3 cluster-mean <= 0: REJECTED, the track is stopped; the designer may only archive it or register a new hypothesis (new registration, section 9); rules are not altered and not retested on the same observations. If S3 cluster-mean > 0: continue to UC2 with no verdict; CI and jackknife are descriptive at UC1.
- UC2 (30 clusters): REJECTED as above if S3 cluster-mean <= 0. NOT ESTABLISHED if S3 mean > 0 but CI95 lower bound <= 0, or removing the largest cluster or the best month leaves a mean <= 0. NOT ESTABLISHED is final for U67-S v1: no extension, no additional clusters, no re-evaluation; any further data requires a new registration. PAPER GATE PASSED only if S3 cluster-mean > 0 AND CI95 lower bound > 0 AND mean without the largest cluster > 0 AND mean without the best month > 0. The paper gate authorizes nothing but a further review; a real-money pilot always needs a separate explicit authorization per track and per direction.
- Tracks are judged on their own. U67-S results are never combined with C11, LONG-M1 or any LONG track; opposite positions on the same symbol in different tracks are not netted. Symbols are disjoint between U67-S and C11/LONG-M1 by construction.
Compatibility note (documented before freeze, as the designer allowed): the C11 protocol treats a non-positive S3 cluster-mean at a checkpoint as a "rejection-review flag" (rules unaltered, designer reviews), while this registration adopts the binding REJECTED wording of the designer-approved, independently audited LONG-M1 registration. This changes the consequence at UC1, not the statistic. It is a registrar choice to be confirmed at the readiness review: if the designer prefers the C11 review-flag semantics he must say so BEFORE deployment, and the registration will be re-frozen as v2 with its own freeze and eligibility literals and a new hash (no outcome data can exist before deployment).
Limits of inference (stated now): clusters are treated as exchangeable in the bootstrap, but adjacent 4H clusters are dependent (the adjacent-merge sensitivity shows this); the confidence interval is conditional on that assumption. The 15-cluster rejection test and the 30-cluster test reuse the first 15 clusters, so no multiplicity correction is made. The universe is the top of one day's turnover ranking, which favors currently active names.

9. CHANGE CONTROL
Any change to rules, parameters, symbols or classification = a new registration with a new counter; old evidence is kept, labeled, never pooled. Code defects are fixed only to restore registered behavior; each fix is documented with its commit and the affected period labeled. No tuning of any kind on observed results.

10. IMPLEMENTATION CONSTRAINTS
Separate files only: crypto_signal_bot_u67_short_v1.py, u67_short_paper_state.json, .github/workflows/paper_u67_short_v1.yml (concurrency group u67-short-paper), own external dispatch job (proposed minutes 19 and 49 of hours 0,4,8,12,16,20 UTC, staggered from C11 :05/:35 and LONG-M1 :12/:42; the schedule is operational, not part of the hypothesis). Engine = copy of the C11 engine with only these differences: SYMS (the frozen 67), STATE_FILE and state/strategy names, eligibility boundary (BEFORE_REGISTRATION_START missed reason), S3 fields (net_r_s3), stop_gap field, R figures dropped from report/log lines. Candidate 11, LONG-M1 and U67-related selection files are not modified. Workflow differences from the C11 workflow (documented): own names/group/paths, and the checkout uses full history (fetch-depth 0), and one guard step before the engine run: if the state file has ever been committed (git history) but is absent in the checkout, the workflow fails instead of letting the engine silently create a new state. Evaluation (cluster building, bootstrap, jackknife, UNRESOLVED rule) is done by a separate evaluator script, not by the engine. Tests use synthetic data only; no historical market data is run through the strategy. Not deployed before the designer's readiness review.

APPENDIX A - CLASSIFICATION EVIDENCE (registered with this text)
Method: (1) every contract symbol named in the KuCoin TradFi announcements retrieved on 2026-10-28 was collected (80 base names; non-exhaustive: KuCoin states 170+ TradFi contracts); (2) the 67 selected symbols were checked against that set: ZERO matches (computed); (3) for the symbols below a crypto-style KuCoin new-listing announcement was located by title search. Symbols without an entry in (3) rest on (2) plus the default rule (no evidence of a non-crypto underlying); the registrar states plainly that for those the evidence is absence of non-crypto evidence, not a positive identification, and that the TradFi announcement set is not guaranteed complete.
Ambiguous-ticker note for the designer's readiness review: the symbols 4, M, S, H, Q, MET, ARC, CAP, APR, WET, BEAT, CLO, ZBT, JCT share their ticker with, or look like, equity or index tickers; for them the registrar has no positive identification beyond the rules above (ARC, CLO, CAP have a crypto listing announcement listed below). The designer may veto any symbol before deployment; a veto means a new version with a new hash.
Announcement sources read: 2026-03-31 (BZUSDT, NATGASUSDT: title only), 2026-04-07 (MU, SNDK), 2026-05-08 (LITE: title only), 2026-05-15, 2026-05-19, 2026-05-22, 2026-06-03, 2026-06-08, 2026-06-10, 2026-06-24, 2026-07-06, 2026-08-19, 2026-09-07, 2026-09-24, 2026-10-05, 2026-10-12, 2026-10-21, 2026-10-27 stock-index perpetual announcements (kucoin.com/announcement/en-kucoin-futures-will-list-multiple-stock-index-perpetual-contracts-<date>), plus CLUSDT new-listing (crude oil) and SPCXUSDT pre-IPO stock-index announcements.

===== BEGIN FILE u67_audit/tests/test_evaluator.py sha256=e86a9b02105777483771994f3871b6c29beebf23d36576f5986bfe8b86db07bd bytes=16092 =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Synthetic-data tests for evaluate_u67.py (no market data, no real state).
import json, os, subprocess, sys, tempfile, hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
EV = os.path.join(HERE, "evaluate_u67.py")
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import evaluate_u67 as E

fails = []
def check(c, msg):
    print(("PASS " if c else "FAIL ") + msg)
    if not c: fails.append(msg)

SF = "u67_short_paper_state.json"
BAR = pd.Timedelta(hours=4)

def run(cmd, repo, env=None):
    e = dict(os.environ); e.update(env or {})
    return subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=repo, capture_output=True, text=True, env=e)

def commit(repo, state, when, msg="s"):
    state["last_run_at"] = str(msg) + str(when)
    with open(os.path.join(repo, SF), "w") as f:
        json.dump(state, f, sort_keys=True)
    run(["git", "add", SF], repo)
    d = pd.Timestamp(when).isoformat()
    r = run(["git", "commit", "-qm", msg], repo, {"GIT_COMMITTER_DATE": d, "GIT_AUTHOR_DATE": d})
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr

def new_repo():
    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    run(["git", "init", "-q"], d); run(["git", "config", "user.name", "t"], d); run(["git", "config", "user.email", "t@t"], d)
    return d

SYMS = ["AAAUSDTM", "BBBUSDTM", "CCCUSDTM", "DDDUSDTM"]
T0 = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-28T12:00:00Z")

def closed_trade(i, sig, sym, reason, bars, entry=10.0, risk_pct=0.02):
    risk = entry * risk_pct
    sl = entry + risk; tp = entry - 2 * risk
    if reason == "SL": gross = -1.0; xp = sl
    elif reason == "TP": gross = 2.0; xp = tp
    else: xp = entry - 0.3 * risk; gross = 0.3
    ent = sig + BAR
    s3c = 0.0015 * (1 + xp / entry) + 0.0001 * bars * 0.5
    return dict(id="%s|%s" % (sym, sig.isoformat()), symbol=sym, side="SHORT", signal_ts=sig.isoformat(), entry_ts=ent.isoformat(),
                entry=entry, risk=risk, risk_pct=risk_pct, sl=sl, tp=tp, bars=bars, status="CLOSED",
                exit_ts=(ent + (bars - 1) * BAR).isoformat(), exit_price=xp, exit_reason=reason, gross_r=gross,
                net_r=gross - 0.002 / risk_pct, net_r_s3=gross - s3c / risk_pct, bars_held=bars, stop_gap=False)

def empty_state(created, eligible):
    return dict(state_version=1, strategy="u67_short_only_frozen_v1", created_at=created, eligible_from=eligible,
                last_run_at=None, symbols={s: dict(last_ts="2026-10-28T08:00:00+00:00", baseline_done=True, last_status="OK") for s in SYMS},
                open={}, closed=[], signals=[], missed=[], errors=[])

def open_pos(sig, sym, entry=10.0, risk_pct=0.02):
    risk = entry * risk_pct
    return dict(id="%s|%s" % (sym, sig.isoformat()), symbol=sym, side="SHORT", signal_ts=sig.isoformat(), entry_ts=(sig + BAR).isoformat(),
                entry=entry, risk=risk, risk_pct=risk_pct, sl=entry + risk, tp=entry - 2 * risk, bars=3, status="OPEN")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- scenario
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
repo = new_repo()
created = "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00"
st = empty_state(created, "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00")
sigs = [T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=8 + 4 * i * 1) for i in range(0, 40)]   # consecutive 4H candles -> exercises adjacent merge
# create 15 clusters (UC1) with 1-3 trades each; make cluster 14 still open at first, close later
plan = []
for i in range(40):
    k = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    for j in range(k):
        plan.append((sigs[i], SYMS[j], rng.choice(["TP", "SL", "TIMEOUT"], p=[.3, .5, .2]), int(rng.integers(2, 31))))
# commit 0: first state
commit(repo, st, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
cur = json.loads(json.dumps(st)); trades_all = []
# progressively add clusters, closing them 1 day after signal; leave cluster index 14 open until a later commit
commit_times = []
for i in range(15):
    for (s, sym, rs, b) in [p for p in plan if p[0] == sigs[i]]:
        if i == 14 and sym == "BBBUSDTM":
            cur["open"][ "%s|%s" % (sym, s.isoformat())] = open_pos(s, sym)
            continue
        tr = closed_trade(0, s, sym, rs, b); cur["closed"].append(tr); trades_all.append(tr)
    when = sigs[i] + pd.Timedelta(hours=5)
    commit(repo, cur, when, "c%d" % i); commit_times.append(when)
# After cluster 14 with an open trade: trigger must NOT hold yet
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "scan"], repo)
out = json.loads(r.stdout)
cluster14_has_b = any(p[0] == sigs[14] and p[1] == "BBBUSDTM" for p in plan)
check(out["trigger_holds"] is (not cluster14_has_b), "trigger respects open trade in cluster 15 (has_open=%s)" % cluster14_has_b)
if cluster14_has_b:
    # close it in a later commit
    s = sigs[14]
    pend = cur["open"].pop("BBBUSDTM|%s" % s.isoformat())
    p = [x for x in plan if x[0] == s and x[1] == "BBBUSDTM"][0]
    tr = closed_trade(0, s, "BBBUSDTM", p[2], p[3]); cur["closed"].append(tr); trades_all.append(tr)
    close_when = sigs[14] + pd.Timedelta(hours=40)
    commit(repo, cur, close_when, "close14")
else:
    close_when = commit_times[-1]
# add extra later clusters (must be excluded from UC1)
for i in range(15, 18):
    for (s, sym, rs, b) in [p for p in plan if p[0] == sigs[i]]:
        tr = closed_trade(0, s, sym, rs, b); cur["closed"].append(tr)
    commit(repo, cur, sigs[i] + pd.Timedelta(hours=5), "late%d" % i)

r1 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "scan"], repo)
o1 = json.loads(r1.stdout)
check(o1["trigger_holds"], "UC1 trigger holds")
check(o1["instant"] == pd.Timestamp(close_when).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "+00:00") or pd.Timestamp(o1["instant"]) == pd.Timestamp(close_when),
      "evaluation instant = commit time of FIRST satisfying commit (not HEAD)")
txt = r1.stdout
check(all(w not in txt for w in ["cluster_mean", "net_r", "S3", "ci95"]), "scan output contains counts only (no R fields)")

# evaluate
r2 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r2.returncode == 0, "evaluate runs")
body = r2.stdout[:r2.stdout.index("RESULT_SHA256")]
res = json.loads(body)
# ---- independent recomputation (plain loops)
sel_sig = sigs[:15]
tr15 = [t for t in trades_all if pd.Timestamp(t["signal_ts"]) in set(sel_sig)]
def indep(scn):
    cm = []
    for s in sel_sig:
        v = []
        for t in tr15:
            if pd.Timestamp(t["signal_ts"]) != s: continue
            e, xp, rp, b, rs = t["entry"], t["exit_price"], t["risk_pct"], t["bars_held"], t["exit_reason"]
            ratio = 1 + xp / e
            if scn == "S0": c = 0.002
            elif scn == "S3": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5
            elif scn == "S3a": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5 + 0.00025 * ratio
            elif scn == "S3b": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5 + 0.0005 * ratio
            elif scn == "S1": c = 0.0006 * ratio
            else: c = (0.0004 if rs == "TP" else 0.0006) * ratio
            v.append(t["gross_r"] - c / rp)
        cm.append(sum(v) / len(v))
    return np.array(cm)
for scn in ["S0", "S3", "S3a", "S3b", "S1", "S2"]:
    cm = indep(scn)
    check(abs(res["stats"][scn]["cluster_mean"] - cm.mean()) < 1e-12, "cluster mean %s matches independent" % scn)
cm3 = indep("S3")
g = np.random.default_rng(20261028); idx = g.integers(0, 15, size=(20000, 15)); mm = cm3[idx].mean(axis=1)
check(abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["ci95_lo"] - np.percentile(mm, 2.5)) < 1e-12 and abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["ci95_hi"] - np.percentile(mm, 97.5)) < 1e-12, "bootstrap CI matches independent (seed 20261028, 20000 draws)")
check(abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["p_mean_gt0"] - (mm > 0).mean()) < 1e-12, "P(mean>0) matches")
# jackknife independent
big = max(range(15), key=lambda i: (cm3[i], -i))   # highest, ties earlier
rem = [cm3[i] for i in range(15) if i != big]
check(abs(res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_largest"] - np.mean(rem)) < 1e-12 and res["jackknife_S3"]["largest_cluster"] == sel_sig[big].isoformat(), "jackknife largest cluster")
months = {}
for i, s in enumerate(sel_sig): months.setdefault(s.strftime("%Y-%m"), []).append(cm3[i])
bm = sorted(months, key=lambda m: (-sum(months[m]), m))[0]
remm = [cm3[i] for i, s in enumerate(sel_sig) if s.strftime("%Y-%m") != bm]
check(res["jackknife_S3"]["best_month"] == bm and (abs(res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_best_month"] - np.mean(remm)) < 1e-12 if remm else res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_best_month"] is None), "jackknife best month")
check(res["n_clusters"] == 15 and res["n_trades"] == len(tr15), "only first 15 clusters, later clusters excluded")
check(res["created_at_check"] == "OK", "created_at check OK")
check(res["verdict"].startswith("REJECTED") == (cm3.mean() <= 0), "UC1 verdict rule consistent with S3 cluster-mean sign")
# adjacent merge (all 15 consecutive -> a single merged group)
check(res["stats"]["S3"]["merged_n"] == 1, "adjacent-merge: 15 consecutive 4H clusters chain into 1 group")
# determinism
r3 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r3.stdout == r2.stdout, "deterministic: byte-identical output on rerun")

# ---- refusal when trigger does not hold
r4 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC2", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r4.returncode == 3 and "cluster_mean" not in r4.stdout, "UC2 evaluate refused (trigger false), no R shown")

# ---- UNRESOLVED rule
repo2 = new_repo()
st2 = empty_state(created, "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00")
commit(repo2, st2, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
cur2 = json.loads(json.dumps(st2))
for i in range(15):
    s = sigs[i]
    if i == 3:
        # open forever on symbol AAA; its cursor stops advancing at 2026-10-28T08:00
        cur2["open"]["AAAUSDTM|%s" % s.isoformat()] = open_pos(s, "AAAUSDTM")
        cur2["closed"].append(closed_trade(0, s, "BBBUSDTM", "SL", 5))
    else:
        cur2["closed"].append(closed_trade(0, s, "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9))
    commit(repo2, cur2, s + pd.Timedelta(hours=5), "u%d" % i)
# not yet 45 days after last_ts -> not triggered
o = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo2, "--checkpoint", "UC1"], repo2).stdout)
check(not o["trigger_holds"], "open position blocks trigger before 45 days")
# commit at last_ts + 44d23h -> still not; +45d -> UNRESOLVED allows trigger
lt = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-28T08:00:00Z")
commit(repo2, cur2, lt + pd.Timedelta(days=44, hours=23), "d44")
o = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo2, "--checkpoint", "UC1"], repo2).stdout)
check(not o["trigger_holds"], "44d23h: not yet UNRESOLVED")
commit(repo2, cur2, lt + pd.Timedelta(days=45), "d45")
o = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo2, "--checkpoint", "UC1"], repo2).stdout)
check(o["trigger_holds"] and pd.Timestamp(o["instant"]) == lt + pd.Timedelta(days=45), "exactly 45d: UNRESOLVED -> trigger holds at that commit")
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo2, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo2)
res2 = json.loads(r.stdout[:r.stdout.index("RESULT_SHA256")])
check(res2["n_unresolved"] == 1, "one UNRESOLVED trade counted")
# check its S3 value: gross -1, exit=sl, bars 30
e = 10.0; rp = 0.02; sl = e * (1 + rp)
exp_s3 = -1.0 - (0.0015 * (1 + sl / e) + 0.0001 * 30 * .5) / rp
exp_s0 = -1.0 - 0.002 / rp
# cluster 3 mean = (that BBB closed SL trade + unresolved)/2
b = closed_trade(0, sigs[3], "BBBUSDTM", "SL", 5)
exp_c3 = (b["net_r_s3"] + exp_s3) / 2
others = []
for i in range(15):
    if i == 3: others.append(exp_c3)
    else: others.append(closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r_s3"])
check(abs(res2["stats"]["S3"]["cluster_mean"] - np.mean(others)) < 1e-12, "UNRESOLVED counted as -1R stop-out with S3 cost, bars 30")
ex = [closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r_s3"] if i != 3 else b["net_r_s3"] for i in range(15)]
check(abs(res2["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] - np.mean(ex)) < 1e-12, "sensitivity excluding UNRESOLVED")
exp_s0c = np.mean([ (b["net_r"] + exp_s0) / 2 if i == 3 else closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r"] for i in range(15)])
check(abs(res2["stats"]["S0"]["cluster_mean"] - exp_s0c) < 1e-12, "UNRESOLVED applies to S0 too")

# ---- created_at mismatch
repo3 = new_repo()
a = empty_state("2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00")
commit(repo3, a, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
c3 = json.loads(json.dumps(a)); c3["created_at"] = "2026-11-02T00:00:00+00:00"; c3["eligible_from"] = "2026-11-02T00:00:00+00:00"
for i in range(15):
    s = pd.Timestamp("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z") + 4 * i * BAR / 4 * 1
    c3["closed"].append(closed_trade(0, s, "AAAUSDTM", "TP", 8))
commit(repo3, c3, pd.Timestamp("2026-11-10T00:00:00Z"), "recreated")
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo3, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo3)
res3 = json.loads(r.stdout[:r.stdout.index("RESULT_SHA256")])
check(res3["created_at_check"].startswith("DEFECT"), "created_at mismatch labelled DEFECT")

# ---- eligibility filter: trade before eligible_from is ignored
repo4 = new_repo()
a = empty_state("2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00")
early = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-28T04:00:00Z")   # entry 08:00 < eligibility
a["closed"].append(closed_trade(0, early, "AAAUSDTM", "TP", 8))
commit(repo4, a, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
o = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo4, "--checkpoint", "UC1"], repo4).stdout)
check(o["counts"]["trades"] == 0, "entry before eligibility boundary is not evidence")

# ---- UC2 decision rule table (unit level)
class A: btc_start = None; btc_end = None
def synth_state(vals_by_cluster, n=30):
    s = empty_state("2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-28T12:05:00+00:00")
    sg = [pd.Timestamp("2026-10-28T20:00:00Z") + pd.Timedelta(days=i) for i in range(n)]
    for i, sig in enumerate(sg):
        tr = closed_trade(0, sig, "AAAUSDTM", "TP" if vals_by_cluster[i] > 0 else "SL", 8)
        s["closed"].append(tr)
    return s, sg
def verdict(vals):
    s, sg = synth_state(vals)
    ct = sg[-1] + pd.Timedelta(days=2)
    return E.evaluate(s, "x", ct, s["created_at"], "UC2", A)
check(verdict([1] * 30)["verdict"] == "PAPER GATE PASSED", "UC2 all winners -> PAPER GATE PASSED")
check(verdict([-1] * 30)["verdict"] == "REJECTED", "UC2 all losers -> REJECTED")
# mean>0 but CI lower bound <= 0: 12 winners (+2R) 18 losers (-1R): mean ~ +0.2.. after costs maybe >0 ; just assert logic consistency
v = verdict([1] * 12 + [-1] * 18)
check(v["verdict"] in ("REJECTED", "NOT ESTABLISHED (final for U67-S v1)"), "UC2 mixed -> REJECTED or NOT ESTABLISHED (never PASSED with CI<=0): " + v["verdict"])

# ---- premature-R guard: the scan/trigger path must never compute any R or cost
def _boom(*a, **k):
    raise AssertionError("cost_fraction called on count-only path")
_orig_cost = E.cost_fraction
E.cost_fraction = _boom
try:
    h1 = E.scan(repo, SF, "UC1")      # trigger holds
    h2 = E.scan(repo, SF, "UC2")      # trigger does not hold
    h3 = E.scan(repo4, SF, "UC1")     # no eligible trades
    ok_scan = (h1[0] is not None) and (h2[0] is None)
except AssertionError as ex:
    ok_scan = False
finally:
    E.cost_fraction = _orig_cost
check(ok_scan, "scan/trigger path computes NO R or cost (cost_fraction patched to raise; trigger true and false cases)")
# CLI refuses evaluate when the trigger is false, shows no R, and has no override flag
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC2", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r.returncode == 3 and "cluster_mean" not in r.stdout + r.stderr and "net" not in r.stdout.lower(), "evaluate refused before trigger: exit 3, no R in output")
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1", "--mode", "evaluate", "--at-commit", "HEAD"], repo)
check(r.returncode != 0, "no override flag exists (unknown option rejected)")
# evaluation commit is the first satisfying commit even when called later with more commits present
o_a = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UC1"], repo).stdout)
check(o_a["first_satisfying_commit"] == o1["first_satisfying_commit"], "first satisfying commit stable")

print("\nFAILS:", fails)
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Synthetic-data tests for evaluate_u67_long.py (no market data, no real state).
import json, os, subprocess, sys, tempfile, hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
EV = os.path.join(HERE, "evaluate_u67_long.py")
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import evaluate_u67_long as E

fails = []
def check(c, msg):
    print(("PASS " if c else "FAIL ") + msg)
    if not c: fails.append(msg)

SF = "u67_long_paper_state.json"
BAR = pd.Timedelta(hours=4)

def run(cmd, repo, env=None):
    e = dict(os.environ); e.update(env or {})
    return subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=repo, capture_output=True, text=True, env=e)

def commit(repo, state, when, msg="s"):
    state["last_run_at"] = str(msg) + str(when)
    with open(os.path.join(repo, SF), "w") as f:
        json.dump(state, f, sort_keys=True)
    run(["git", "add", SF], repo)
    d = pd.Timestamp(when).isoformat()
    r = run(["git", "commit", "-qm", msg], repo, {"GIT_COMMITTER_DATE": d, "GIT_AUTHOR_DATE": d})
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr

def new_repo():
    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    run(["git", "init", "-q"], d); run(["git", "config", "user.name", "t"], d); run(["git", "config", "user.email", "t@t"], d)
    return d

SYMS = ["AAAUSDTM", "BBBUSDTM", "CCCUSDTM", "DDDUSDTM"]
T0 = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-30T12:00:00Z")

def closed_trade(i, sig, sym, reason, bars, entry=10.0, risk_pct=0.02):
    risk = entry * risk_pct
    sl = entry - risk; tp = entry + 2 * risk
    if reason == "SL": gross = -1.0; xp = sl
    elif reason == "TP": gross = 2.0; xp = tp
    else: xp = entry + 0.3 * risk; gross = 0.3
    ent = sig + BAR
    s3c = 0.0015 * (1 + xp / entry) + 0.0001 * bars * 0.5
    return dict(id="%s|%s" % (sym, sig.isoformat()), symbol=sym, side="LONG", signal_ts=sig.isoformat(), entry_ts=ent.isoformat(),
                entry=entry, risk=risk, risk_pct=risk_pct, sl=sl, tp=tp, bars=bars, status="CLOSED",
                exit_ts=(ent + (bars - 1) * BAR).isoformat(), exit_price=xp, exit_reason=reason, gross_r=gross,
                net_r=gross - 0.002 / risk_pct, net_r_s3=gross - s3c / risk_pct, bars_held=bars, stop_gap=False)

def empty_state(created, eligible):
    return dict(state_version=1, strategy="u67_short_only_frozen_v1", created_at=created, eligible_from=eligible,
                last_run_at=None, symbols={s: dict(last_ts="2026-10-30T08:00:00+00:00", baseline_done=True, last_status="OK") for s in SYMS},
                open={}, closed=[], signals=[], missed=[], errors=[])

def open_pos(sig, sym, entry=10.0, risk_pct=0.02):
    risk = entry * risk_pct
    return dict(id="%s|%s" % (sym, sig.isoformat()), symbol=sym, side="LONG", signal_ts=sig.isoformat(), entry_ts=(sig + BAR).isoformat(),
                entry=entry, risk=risk, risk_pct=risk_pct, sl=entry - risk, tp=entry + 2 * risk, bars=3, status="OPEN")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- scenario
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
repo = new_repo()
created = "2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00"
st = empty_state(created, "2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00")
sigs = [T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=8 + 4 * i * 1) for i in range(0, 40)]   # consecutive 4H candles -> exercises adjacent merge
# create 15 clusters (UL1) with 1-3 trades each; make cluster 14 still open at first, close later
plan = []
for i in range(40):
    k = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    for j in range(k):
        plan.append((sigs[i], SYMS[j], rng.choice(["TP", "SL", "TIMEOUT"], p=[.3, .5, .2]), int(rng.integers(2, 31))))
# commit 0: first state
commit(repo, st, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
cur = json.loads(json.dumps(st)); trades_all = []
# progressively add clusters, closing them 1 day after signal; leave cluster index 14 open until a later commit
commit_times = []
for i in range(15):
    for (s, sym, rs, b) in [p for p in plan if p[0] == sigs[i]]:
        if i == 14 and sym == "BBBUSDTM":
            cur["open"][ "%s|%s" % (sym, s.isoformat())] = open_pos(s, sym)
            continue
        tr = closed_trade(0, s, sym, rs, b); cur["closed"].append(tr); trades_all.append(tr)
    when = sigs[i] + pd.Timedelta(hours=5)
    commit(repo, cur, when, "c%d" % i); commit_times.append(when)
# After cluster 14 with an open trade: trigger must NOT hold yet
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL1", "--mode", "scan"], repo)
out = json.loads(r.stdout)
cluster14_has_b = any(p[0] == sigs[14] and p[1] == "BBBUSDTM" for p in plan)
check(out["trigger_holds"] is (not cluster14_has_b), "trigger respects open trade in cluster 15 (has_open=%s)" % cluster14_has_b)
if cluster14_has_b:
    # close it in a later commit
    s = sigs[14]
    pend = cur["open"].pop("BBBUSDTM|%s" % s.isoformat())
    p = [x for x in plan if x[0] == s and x[1] == "BBBUSDTM"][0]
    tr = closed_trade(0, s, "BBBUSDTM", p[2], p[3]); cur["closed"].append(tr); trades_all.append(tr)
    close_when = sigs[14] + pd.Timedelta(hours=40)
    commit(repo, cur, close_when, "close14")
else:
    close_when = commit_times[-1]
# add extra later clusters (must be excluded from UL1)
for i in range(15, 18):
    for (s, sym, rs, b) in [p for p in plan if p[0] == sigs[i]]:
        tr = closed_trade(0, s, sym, rs, b); cur["closed"].append(tr)
    commit(repo, cur, sigs[i] + pd.Timedelta(hours=5), "late%d" % i)

r1 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL1", "--mode", "scan"], repo)
o1 = json.loads(r1.stdout)
check(o1["trigger_holds"], "UL1 trigger holds")
check(o1["instant"] == pd.Timestamp(close_when).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "+00:00") or pd.Timestamp(o1["instant"]) == pd.Timestamp(close_when),
      "evaluation instant = commit time of FIRST satisfying commit (not HEAD)")
txt = r1.stdout
check(all(w not in txt for w in ["cluster_mean", "net_r", "S3", "ci95"]), "scan output contains counts only (no R fields)")

# evaluate
r2 = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r2.returncode == 0, "evaluate runs")
body = r2.stdout[:r2.stdout.index("RESULT_SHA256")]
res = json.loads(body)
# ---- independent recomputation (plain loops)
sel_sig = sigs[:15]
tr15 = [t for t in trades_all if pd.Timestamp(t["signal_ts"]) in set(sel_sig)]
def indep(scn):
    cm = []
    for s in sel_sig:
        v = []
        for t in tr15:
            if pd.Timestamp(t["signal_ts"]) != s: continue
            e, xp, rp, b, rs = t["entry"], t["exit_price"], t["risk_pct"], t["bars_held"], t["exit_reason"]
            ratio = 1 + xp / e
            if scn == "S0": c = 0.002
            elif scn == "S3": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5
            elif scn == "S3a": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5 + 0.00025 * ratio
            elif scn == "S3b": c = 0.0015 * ratio + 0.0001 * b * .5 + 0.0005 * ratio
            elif scn == "S1": c = 0.0006 * ratio
            else: c = (0.0004 if rs == "TP" else 0.0006) * ratio
            v.append(t["gross_r"] - c / rp)
        cm.append(sum(v) / len(v))
    return np.array(cm)
for scn in ["S0", "S3", "S3a", "S3b", "S1", "S2"]:
    cm = indep(scn)
    check(abs(res["stats"][scn]["cluster_mean"] - cm.mean()) < 1e-12, "cluster mean %s matches independent" % scn)
cm3 = indep("S3")
g = np.random.default_rng(20261029); idx = g.integers(0, 15, size=(20000, 15)); mm = cm3[idx].mean(axis=1)
check(abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["ci95_lo"] - np.percentile(mm, 2.5)) < 1e-12 and abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["ci95_hi"] - np.percentile(mm, 97.5)) < 1e-12, "bootstrap CI matches independent (seed 20261029, 20000 draws)")
check(abs(res["stats"]["S3"]["p_mean_gt0"] - (mm > 0).mean()) < 1e-12, "P(mean>0) matches")
# jackknife independent
big = max(range(15), key=lambda i: (cm3[i], -i))   # highest, ties earlier
rem = [cm3[i] for i in range(15) if i != big]
check(abs(res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_largest"] - np.mean(rem)) < 1e-12 and res["jackknife_S3"]["largest_cluster"] == sel_sig[big].isoformat(), "jackknife largest cluster")
months = {}
for i, s in enumerate(sel_sig): months.setdefault(s.strftime("%Y-%m"), []).append(cm3[i])
bm = sorted(months, key=lambda m: (-sum(months[m]), m))[0]
remm = [cm3[i] for i, s in enumerate(sel_sig) if s.strftime("%Y-%m") != bm]
check(res["jackknife_S3"]["best_month"] == bm and (abs(res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_best_month"] - np.mean(remm)) < 1e-12 if remm else res["jackknife_S3"]["mean_without_best_month"] is None), "jackknife best month")
check(res["n_clusters"] == 15 and res["n_trades"] == len(tr15), "only first 15 clusters, later clusters excluded")
check(res["created_at_check"] == "OK", "created_at check OK")
check(res["verdict"].startswith("REJECTED") == (cm3.mean() <= 0), "UL1 verdict rule consistent with S3 cluster-mean sign")
# adjacent merge (all 15 consecutive -> a single merged group)

others = []
for i in range(15):
    if i == 3: others.append(exp_c3)
    else: others.append(closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r_s3"])
check(abs(res2["stats"]["S3"]["cluster_mean"] - np.mean(others)) < 1e-12, "UNRESOLVED counted as -1R stop-out with S3 cost, bars 30")
ex = [closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r_s3"] if i != 3 else b["net_r_s3"] for i in range(15)]
check(abs(res2["S3_cluster_mean_excluding_unresolved"] - np.mean(ex)) < 1e-12, "sensitivity excluding UNRESOLVED")
exp_s0c = np.mean([ (b["net_r"] + exp_s0) / 2 if i == 3 else closed_trade(0, sigs[i], "BBBUSDTM", "TP", 9)["net_r"] for i in range(15)])
check(abs(res2["stats"]["S0"]["cluster_mean"] - exp_s0c) < 1e-12, "UNRESOLVED applies to S0 too")

# ---- created_at mismatch
repo3 = new_repo()
a = empty_state("2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00")
commit(repo3, a, T0 + pd.Timedelta(hours=6), "first")
c3 = json.loads(json.dumps(a)); c3["created_at"] = "2026-11-02T00:00:00+00:00"; c3["eligible_from"] = "2026-11-02T00:00:00+00:00"
for i in range(15):
    s = pd.Timestamp("2026-11-02T04:00:00Z") + 4 * i * BAR / 4 * 1
    c3["closed"].append(closed_trade(0, s, "AAAUSDTM", "TP", 8))
commit(repo3, c3, pd.Timestamp("2026-11-10T00:00:00Z"), "recreated")
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo3, "--checkpoint", "UL1", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo3)
res3 = json.loads(r.stdout[:r.stdout.index("RESULT_SHA256")])
check(res3["created_at_check"].startswith("DEFECT"), "created_at mismatch labelled DEFECT")

# ---- eligibility filter: trade before eligible_from is ignored
repo4 = new_repo()
a = empty_state("2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00")
early = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-30T04:00:00Z")   # entry 08:00 < eligibility

# ---- UL2 decision rule table (unit level)
class A: btc_start = None; btc_end = None
def synth_state(vals_by_cluster, n=30):
    s = empty_state("2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00", "2026-10-30T12:05:00+00:00")
    sg = [pd.Timestamp("2026-10-30T20:00:00Z") + pd.Timedelta(days=i) for i in range(n)]
    for i, sig in enumerate(sg):
        tr = closed_trade(0, sig, "AAAUSDTM", "TP" if vals_by_cluster[i] > 0 else "SL", 8)
        s["closed"].append(tr)
    return s, sg
def verdict(vals):
    s, sg = synth_state(vals)
    ct = sg[-1] + pd.Timedelta(days=2)
    return E.evaluate(s, "x", ct, s["created_at"], "UL2", A)
check(verdict([1] * 30)["verdict"] == "PAPER GATE PASSED", "UL2 all winners -> PAPER GATE PASSED")
check(verdict([-1] * 30)["verdict"] == "REJECTED", "UL2 all losers -> REJECTED")
# mean>0 but CI lower bound <= 0: 12 winners (+2R) 18 losers (-1R): mean ~ +0.2.. after costs maybe >0 ; just assert logic consistency
v = verdict([1] * 12 + [-1] * 18)
check(v["verdict"] in ("REJECTED", "NOT ESTABLISHED (final for U67-L v1)"), "UL2 mixed -> REJECTED or NOT ESTABLISHED (never PASSED with CI<=0): " + v["verdict"])

# ---- premature-R guard: the scan/trigger path must never compute any R or cost
def _boom(*a, **k):
    raise AssertionError("cost_fraction called on count-only path")
_orig_cost = E.cost_fraction
E.cost_fraction = _boom
try:
    h1 = E.scan(repo, SF, "UL1")      # trigger holds
    h2 = E.scan(repo, SF, "UL2")      # trigger does not hold
    h3 = E.scan(repo4, SF, "UL1")     # no eligible trades
    ok_scan = (h1[0] is not None) and (h2[0] is None)
except AssertionError as ex:
    ok_scan = False
finally:
    E.cost_fraction = _orig_cost
check(ok_scan, "scan/trigger path computes NO R or cost (cost_fraction patched to raise; trigger true and false cases)")
# CLI refuses evaluate when the trigger is false, shows no R, and has no override flag
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL2", "--mode", "evaluate"], repo)
check(r.returncode == 3 and "cluster_mean" not in r.stdout + r.stderr and "net" not in r.stdout.lower(), "evaluate refused before trigger: exit 3, no R in output")
r = run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL1", "--mode", "evaluate", "--at-commit", "HEAD"], repo)
check(r.returncode != 0, "no override flag exists (unknown option rejected)")
# evaluation commit is the first satisfying commit even when called later with more commits present
o_a = json.loads(run([sys.executable, EV, "--repo", repo, "--checkpoint", "UL1"], repo).stdout)
check(o_a["first_satisfying_commit"] == o1["first_satisfying_commit"], "first satisfying commit stable")

print("\nFAILS:", fails)
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)

# ---------- 2 synth + equivalence ----------
def synth(seed,n,start="2020-01-01"):
    r=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ret=r.standard_t(3,n)*0.012+0.0004
    close=100*np.exp(np.cumsum(ret))
    op=np.r_[close[0],close[:-1]]*(1+r.normal(0,0.002,n))
    hi=np.maximum(op,close)*(1+np.abs(r.normal(0,0.006,n)))
    lo=np.minimum(op,close)*(1-np.abs(r.normal(0,0.006,n)))
    vol=np.exp(r.normal(10,0.6,n))
    ts=pd.date_range(start,periods=n,freq="4h",tz="UTC")
    return pd.DataFrame(dict(ts=ts,open=op,high=hi,low=lo,close=close,volume=vol))
X=synth(1,1500)

for seed in range(2,14):
